    package '02_Functional' {
        package '00_UseCaseDefinitions' {
            use case def ReviewAndApproveStepResult;
            use case def AssessLegacyData;
            use case def PrepareDataForSynthesis;
            use case def ApplySemanticGuardrails;
            use case def ResolveAmbiguity;
            use case def SynthesizeSysMLv2Model;
            use case def ValidateGeneratedArchitecture;

            use case def ReviewAndAuthorizeFinalModel;
        }
        package '01_CoreUseCases' {
            use case <UC_000> : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::ReviewAndApproveStepResult {
                doc
                /* Goal
                 * Ensure human supervision of automated intermediate processing results.
                 *
                 * Preconditions
                 * A processing step has generated one or more candidate results
                 * requiring human review.
                 *
                 * Main Success Scenario
                 * 1. The system presents the candidate result and its applicable
                 *    explanation and traceability information.
                 * 2. The user reviews the presented result.
                 * 3. The user modifies or selects the preferred result where applicable.
                 * 4. The system records the explicit human review decision.
                 * 5. An approved intermediate result may be used by the explicitly
                 *    defined subsequent processing step.
                 *
                 * Postconditions
                 * A human-reviewed intermediate result and its review decision are
                 * available.
                 *
                 * This use case establishes authority only for the reviewed intermediate
                 * result within its defined processing context. It does not establish
                 * Final Model Authority.
                 */
                dependency from UC_000 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Expert Review of Generated Interpretations';
                dependency from UC_000 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Human Intervention for Conflicts';
                dependency from UC_000 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Explanation of Generated Engineering Results';
                dependency from UC_000 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Model Content Validation';
                subject ;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_01;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_02;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_03;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_04;
                objective {
                    doc
                    /* Review, adjust and explicitly authorize an intermediate result
                     * for its defined subsequent processing context.
                     */
                }
                item 'input: CandidateStepResult';
                item 'output: ReviewedIntermediateResult';
            }
            use case <UC_001> : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::AssessLegacyData {
                doc
                /* Goal
                 * Assess the quality and suitability of legacy engineering data.
                 *
                 * Preconditions
                 * Legacy engineering data is available.
                 *
                 * Main Success Scenario
                 * 1. The user selects legacy engineering data.
                 * 2. The system evaluates completeness.
                 * 3. The system evaluates consistency.
                 * 4. The system evaluates reliability and relevance.
                 * 5. The system generates an assessment report.
                 * 6. The user reviews and approves the assessment report.
                 *
                 * Postconditions
                 * Validated assessment information is available for subsequent processing.*/
                include use case reviewAndApprove ::> UC_000;
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Input Data Suitability Assessment';
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Input Data Completeness Check';
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Input Data Consistency Check';
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Source Reliability Classification';
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Source Relevance Classification';
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Input Constraint Violation Detection';
                dependency from UC_001 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Legacy Data Completion';
                subject ;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_01;
                objective {
                    doc /* Assess the suitability of legacy engineering data for automated processing.*/
                }
                doc /**/
                item 'input: LegacyEngineeringData' {
                    doc /**/
                }
                item 'output: DataAssessmentReport';
            }
            use case <UC_002> : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::PrepareDataForSynthesis {
                doc
                /* Goal
                 * Ensure that legacy engineering data is complete and suitable for synthesis.
                 *
                 * Preconditions
                 * The assessment report has been approved.

            use case <UC_005> : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::SynthesizeSysMLv2Model {
                doc /*
                * Goal
                * Synthesize a traceable SysML v2 model representation from engineering
                * information admitted to the current project.
                *
                * Preconditions
                * Source-local engineering information required for synthesis has
                * completed its applicable Human Review.
                * The applicable source contributions have passed the Project Fit gate.
                *
                * Main Success Scenario
                * 1. The user initiates model synthesis.
                * 2. The system derives one Model Candidate Set from the admitted
                *    engineering information.
                * 3. The system preserves the contribution and traceability of each
                *    originating engineering source.
                * 4. The system structures the generated candidate content according to
                *    the applicable target-model structure.
                * 5. The Model Candidate Set is presented for the required intermediate
                *    Human Review.
                * 6. The user reviews and approves the applicable candidate content for
                *    subsequent model synthesis.
                * 7. The system assembles the human-authorized candidate content into the
                *    resulting model representation.
                * 8. The system generates the corresponding SysML v2 textual artifact set.
                *
                * Postconditions
                * A Generated SysML Artifact Set is available for automated validation.
                * No Final Model Authority or publication authority has been established.
                */

                include use case reviewAndApprove ::> UC_000;

                dependency from UC_005 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '01_Functional'::'Architecture Information Derivation';
                dependency from UC_005 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '02_Architectural'::'Structural Pattern Conformance';
                dependency from UC_005 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '02_Architectural'::'Model Reusability';
                dependency from UC_005 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '02_Architectural'::'Modular Model Growth';
                dependency from UC_005 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '02_Architectural'::'Non Destructive Submodel Integration';
                dependency from UC_005 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '01_Functional'::'Source Traceability';
                dependency from UC_005 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '02_Architectural'::'Reusable Architectural Pattern Application';
                dependency from UC_005 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '01_Functional'::'Multiple Source Support';
                dependency from UC_005 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '01_Functional'::'Architecture as Code Provision';
                dependency from UC_005 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '01_Functional'::'SysML v2 Target Representation';

                subject ;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_01;
                objective {
                    doc /*
                    Generate a traceable SysML v2 artifact set from Project-Fit-admitted
                    engineering information and human-authorized model candidates.
                    */
                }
                item 'input: AdmittedEngineeringInformation';
                item 'output: GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet';
            }
            use case <UC_006> : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::ValidateGeneratedArchitecture {
                doc /*
                * Goal
                * Validate the generated SysML v2 artifact set and provide explicit
                * validation evidence for Final Model Review.
                *
                * Preconditions
                * A Generated SysML Artifact Set is available.
                *
                * Main Success Scenario
                * 1. The system validates the integrity and consistency of the generated
                *    artifact set.
                * 2. The system validates conformance to the applicable SysML v2 textual
                *    notation rules.
                * 3. The system validates the applicable artifact-structure and
                *    generation-policy constraints.
                * 4. The system validates relationship, endpoint and traceability
                *    consistency within the generated artifact set.
                * 5. The system records all validation findings in an explicit validation
                *    result.
                *
                * Postconditions
                * A SysML Validation Result is available for Final Model Review.
                * Validation does not modify generated model content.
                * Validation does not establish Final Model Authority.
                */

                dependency from UC_006 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '04_Assurance'::'Model Content Validation';
                dependency from UC_006 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '01_Functional'::'SysML v2 Target Representation';
                dependency from UC_006 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '01_Functional'::'Architecture as Code Provision';
                dependency from UC_006 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '01_Functional'::'Source Traceability';
                dependency from UC_006 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '04_Assurance'::'Explanation of Generated Engineering Results';
                subject ;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_03;
                objective {
                    doc /*
                    Validate the generated SysML v2 artifact set and provide explicit
                    validation evidence without modifying the generated model or
                    establishing model authority.
                    */
                }
                item 'input: GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet';
                item 'output: SysMLValidationResult';
            }
            use case <UC_007> : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::AssessProjectSourceAdmissibility {
                doc
                /* Goal
                 * Determine whether the current reviewed engineering source
                 * contributions are admissible for use within the active engineering
                 * project.
                 *
                 * Preconditions
                 * Current source-local reviewed engineering information is available
                 * for the applicable engineering sources.
                 *
                 * Main Success Scenario
                 * 1. The system identifies the current reviewed engineering sources.
                 * 2. The system assesses the applicability of each source contribution
                 *    to the active engineering project.
                 * 3. The system preserves the exact project, source and processing
                 *    context of each assessment.
                 * 4. The system identifies source contributions requiring explicit
                 *    Human resolution.
                 * 5. The Information Manager resolves such cases where required.
                 * 6. The system records the resulting Project Fit state.
                 *
                 * Postconditions
                 * The admissibility state of each applicable current engineering source
                 * contribution is known.
                 * Only admitted contributions may proceed to project-level model
                 * synthesis.
                 *
                 * Automatic cross-source semantic reconciliation is not part of this
                 * use case.
                 */
                dependency from UC_007 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'Multiple Source Support';
                dependency from UC_007 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'Source Traceability';
                dependency from UC_007 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Source Relevance Classification';
                dependency from UC_007 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'02_Architectural'::'Project Information Isolation';
                dependency from UC_007 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Human Review Decision Association';
                dependency from UC_007 to '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Approved Information Boundary';
                subject ;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_04;
                objective {
                    doc
                    /* Establish the project admissibility of current reviewed source
                     * contributions before model synthesis.
                     */
                }
                item 'input: SourceLocalReviewedEngineeringInformation';
                item 'output: ProjectFitState';
            }
            use case <UC_008>
                : '00_UseCaseDefinitions'::ReviewAndAuthorizeFinalModel {

                doc /*
                * Goal
                * Perform the final Human review of the complete generated SysML v2 model
                * representation and explicitly establish Final Model Authority.
                *
                * Preconditions
                * A Generated SysML Artifact Set and its corresponding SysML Validation
                * Result are available.
                *
                * Main Success Scenario
                * 1. The system presents the Generated SysML Artifact Set.
                * 2. The system presents the corresponding validation findings,
                *    explanation and traceability information.
                * 3. The System Architect and System Modeler review the complete generated
                *    model representation.
                * 4. The reviewers assess identified validation findings and the
                *    engineering content represented by the generated model.
                * 5. The reviewers reject the result or request correction where the
                *    generated model is not acceptable.
                * 6. The reviewers record an explicit Final Model Review decision.
                * 7. If the exact reviewed artifact set is approved and all mandatory
                *    publication conditions are satisfied, Final Model Authority is
                *    established for that reviewed model revision.
                *
                * Postconditions
                * An explicit Final Model Review decision exists for the exact reviewed
                * Generated SysML Artifact Set.
                * An approved decision establishes Final Model Authority only for that
                * exact reviewed model revision.
                * Final publication remains prohibited unless the applicable validation
                * and release conditions are satisfied.
                */
                dependency from UC_008 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '04_Assurance'::'Model Content Validation';
                dependency from UC_008 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '04_Assurance'::'Human Review Decision Association';
                dependency from UC_008 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '04_Assurance'::'Approved Information Boundary';
                dependency from UC_008 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '04_Assurance'::'Explanation of Generated Engineering Results';
                dependency from UC_008 to
                    '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::
                    '01_Functional'::'Source Traceability';
                subject ;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_03;
                actor : '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_01;
                objective {
                    doc /*
                    Review and explicitly authorize the exact generated SysML v2 model
                    revision before final engineering publication.
                    */
                }
                item 'input: GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet';
                item 'input: SysMLValidationResult';
                item 'output: FinalModelReviewDecision';
            }
        }
        package '02_Views' {
            view GV_UseCaseOverview : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::gv {
                doc
                /* Overview of the Stakeholder-level functional use cases and
                 * participating human roles of the Turing Generator.
                 *
                 * The view represents stakeholder interaction and functional intent.
                 * It does not define System Functions, Logical Components or
                 * subsystem boundaries.
                 */
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_000;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_001;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_002;

        package '03_Traceability' {
            view UC_SR_Traceability : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::gv {
                doc
                /* Traceability view between Stakeholder-level use cases and
                 * Stakeholder Requirements.
                 *
                 * Use Case dependencies represent the Stakeholder Requirements
                 * addressed by each interaction scenario.
                 *
                 * The traceability represented here is stakeholder-level functional
                 * traceability and shall not be interpreted as System Function or
                 * subsystem allocation.
                 */
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_000;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_001;
                expose '01_CoreUseCases'::UC_002;

                expose '01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::*;
            }
        }
    }
    package '03_Logical' {
        doc
        /* Stakeholder Logical modeling note:
         * This package defines the external logical context of the Turing Generator
         * as the System of Interest.
         * It represents the human stakeholder roles, external engineering source
         * information and stakeholder-visible information exchanges across the
         * system boundary.
         * It does not define internal System Functions, Logical Components,
         * subsystems, software modules, technologies or deployment structures.
         */
        package '00_ContextDefinitions' {
            part def ContextElement;
            port def ContextInteractionPort;
            interface def ContextInteraction {
                end sourceEnd : ContextInteractionPort;
                end targetEnd : ContextInteractionPort;
            }
            part def StakeholderContextActor :> ContextElement {
                doc
                /* Logical occurrence of an existing human stakeholder role
                 * within the Turing Generator system context.
                 */
                port systemPort : ContextInteractionPort;
            }
            part def 'Engineering Source' :> ContextElement {
                doc
                /* External engineering source artifact or source-information
                 * context registered for processing by the Turing Generator.
                 */
                port systemPort : ContextInteractionPort;
            }
            part def 'Turing Generator System' :> ContextElement {
                doc
                /* System of Interest that transforms heterogeneous legacy
                 * engineering information into traceable SysML v2 architecture
                 * artifacts under explicit human review and authority.
                 */
                port sourceInformationPort : ContextInteractionPort;
                port modelerInteractionPort : ContextInteractionPort;
                port domainInteractionPort : ContextInteractionPort;
                port architectureInteractionPort : ContextInteractionPort;
                port informationGovernancePort : ContextInteractionPort;
            }
        }
        package '01_SystemContext' {
            part <CTX_001> 'Turing Generator Stakeholder Context' {
                doc
                /* External logical context of the Turing Generator.
                 * The Turing Generator is the System of Interest.
                 * Human stakeholder roles and Engineering Sources remain outside
                 * the system boundary.
                 * The represented interactions describe stakeholder-visible
                 * information exchange and authority interaction only.
                 */
                part turingGenerator : '00_ContextDefinitions'::'Turing Generator System';
                part engineeringSources : '00_ContextDefinitions'::'Engineering Source';
                part systemModeler : '00_ContextDefinitions'::StakeholderContextActor;
                part domainExpert : '00_ContextDefinitions'::StakeholderContextActor;
                part systemArchitect : '00_ContextDefinitions'::StakeholderContextActor;
                part informationManager : '00_ContextDefinitions'::StakeholderContextActor;
                dependency from systemModeler to '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_01;
                dependency from domainExpert to '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_02;
                dependency from systemArchitect to '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_03;
                dependency from informationManager to '00_Stakeholders'::ACT_04;
                interface engineeringSourceExchange : '00_ContextDefinitions'::ContextInteraction
                        connect sourceEnd ::> engineeringSources.systemPort
                        to targetEnd ::> turingGenerator.sourceInformationPort;

                interface modelerInteraction : '00_ContextDefinitions'::ContextInteraction
                        connect sourceEnd ::> systemModeler.systemPort
                        to targetEnd ::> turingGenerator.modelerInteractionPort;

                interface domainClarificationInteraction : '00_ContextDefinitions'::ContextInteraction
                        connect sourceEnd ::> domainExpert.systemPort
                        to targetEnd ::> turingGenerator.domainInteractionPort;

                interface architectureReviewAndAuthorityInteraction : '00_ContextDefinitions'::ContextInteraction
                        connect sourceEnd ::> systemArchitect.systemPort to targetEnd ::> turingGenerator.architectureInteractionPort;

                interface informationGovernanceInteraction : '00_ContextDefinitions'::ContextInteraction
                        connect sourceEnd ::> informationManager.systemPort
                        to targetEnd ::> turingGenerator.informationGovernancePort;
            }
        }
        package '02_Views' {
            view IV_TuringGeneratorStakeholderContext : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::iv {
                doc
                /* Stakeholder context diagram of the Turing Generator.
                 * The view exposes the System of Interest together with the
                 * human stakeholder roles and external Engineering Sources.
                 * Context interactions represent information and authority
                 * exchange across the system boundary.
                 * Internal System Functions, Logical Components and subsystem
                 * structures are intentionally not shown.
                 */
                expose '01_SystemContext'::CTX_001::turingGenerator;
                expose '01_SystemContext'::CTX_001::engineeringSources;
                expose '01_SystemContext'::CTX_001::systemModeler;
                expose '01_SystemContext'::CTX_001::domainExpert;
                expose '01_SystemContext'::CTX_001::systemArchitect;
                expose '01_SystemContext'::CTX_001::informationManager;
            }
        }
    }

package '02_System' {
    package '01_Requirements' {
        doc
        /* System Requirement modeling note:
         *
         * The System Requirements were derived individually from the accepted Stakeholder Requirements.
         * The topical package grouping was applied retrospectively for navigation and documentation only.
         * It shall not be interpreted as a pre-allocation to Logical Components or as a definition of subsystem boundaries.
         * System Functions and Logical Components are derived only after this System Requirement baseline is accepted.
         */
        package '00_RequirementDefinitions' {
            requirement def BaseSystemRequirement {
                attribute rationale;
                attribute source;
                attribute scope;
            }
            requirement def SystemRequirement :> BaseSystemRequirement;
            requirement def DataSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
            requirement def FunctionalSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
            requirement def AssuranceSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
            requirement def SemanticSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
            requirement def ArchitectureSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
            requirement def ValidationSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
            requirement def UserInterfaceSystemRequirement :> SystemRequirement;
        }
        package '01_InputSuitabilityAndQuality' {
            requirement <SYSR_001> 'Assess Source Processing Suitability' : '00_RequirementDefinitions'::DataSystemRequirement {
                doc /* The system shall determine whether each registered engineering source is suitable for the selected automated processing task based on the applicable processing and information-quality criteria. */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /* Establishes a task-specific suitability decision that goes beyond technical file readability. */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /* SR_001 Input Data Suitability Assessment */
                }
                attribute :>> scope {
                    doc /* mvp */
                }
                dependency
                        from 'Assess Source Processing Suitability'
                        to '01_Stakeholder'::'01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Input Data Suitability Assessment';
            }
            requirement <SYSR_002> 'Extract Processable Source Information' : '00_RequirementDefinitions'::FunctionalSystemRequirement {
                doc /*
                The system shall create a deterministic, persistable and
                source-traceable processable projection of each supported registered
                engineering source before semantic processing.
                */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /*
                    Establishes a reproducible machine-processable representation while
                    preserving the relationship to the registered source and separating
                    deterministic source preparation from semantic interpretation.
                    */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /*
                    SR_001 Input Data Suitability Assessment;
                    SR_019 Source Traceability;

                    Changed due to ADR-011 Semantic Information Unit and Ontology Boundary
                        */
                }
                attribute :>> scope {
                    doc /* mvp */
                }
                dependency from 'Extract Processable Source Information' to
                    '01_Stakeholder'::'01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Input Data Suitability Assessment';
                dependency from 'Extract Processable Source Information' to
                    '01_Stakeholder'::'01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'Source Traceability';
            }
            requirement <SYSR_006> 'Identify Incomplete Input Information' : '00_RequirementDefinitions'::DataSystemRequirement {
                doc /* The system shall identify information required for the selected processing task that is missing from the assessed source information. */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /* Defines completeness relative to the selected engineering task rather than as an absolute property of a source. */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /* SR_002 Input Data Completeness Check */
                }
                attribute :>> scope {
                    doc /* mvp */
                }

            }
            requirement <SYSR_040> 'Identify Inconsistent Input Information' : '00_RequirementDefinitions'::DataSystemRequirement {
                doc /*
                The system shall identify contradictory or mutually inconsistent
                information within the source-local engineering information assessed
                for the selected processing task.
                */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /*
                    Performs consistency assessment within one source-processing context
                    without implying automatic cross-source semantic reconciliation.
                    */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /* SR_003 Input Data Consistency Check
                                * Changed due to ADR-032 Project-Level Multi-Source Reconciliation and Controlled Engineering Evolution
                                *    - cross-source reconciliation separated from source-local consistency assessment

                    doc /* mvp */
                }

                    '01_Stakeholder'::'01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Input Data Consistency Check';
            }
            requirement <SYSR_041> 'Classify Source Information Reliability' : '00_RequirementDefinitions'::DataSystemRequirement {
                doc /*
                The system shall assign each assessed source information item a
                reliability classification according to the applicable reliability
                criteria.
                */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /*
                    Makes source reliability explicit and reviewable without prescribing
                    classification values.
                    */

                    doc /* SR_004 Source Reliability Classification */

                    doc /* target */

            }
            requirement <SYSR_042> 'Classify Source Information Relevance' : '00_RequirementDefinitions'::DataSystemRequirement {
                doc /* The system shall assign each assessed source information item a relevance classification relative to the selected engineering task. */
                attribute :>> rationale {

                attribute :>> rationale {

                }

            }
            requirement <SYSR_118> 'Preserve Engineering Source Purity' : '00_RequirementDefinitions'::AssuranceSystemRequirement {
                doc /*
                The system shall prevent reference knowledge, context-only information,
                processing instructions, orchestration information and other
                non-authoritative context from becoming positive Project engineering
                evidence.
                */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /*
                    Ensures that contextual guidance may support processing and
                    interpretation without being mistaken for engineering information
                    established by a registered Engineering Source.
                    */
                }
                attribute :>> source {

                    */

                    doc /* mvp */
                }

                dependency from 'Preserve Engineering Source Purity' to
                    '01_Stakeholder'::'01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'04_Assurance'::'Approved Information Boundary';
            }
            requirement <SYSR_119> 'Assess Project Source Admissibility' : '00_RequirementDefinitions'::AssuranceSystemRequirement {
                doc /*
                The system shall assess whether each applicable processed registered
                source is admissible for engineering use within the active Project
                context before that source contributes to project-level model synthesis.
                */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /*
                    Introduces an explicit Project Fit assessment so that registration
                    within a Project alone does not authorize a source contribution for
                    project-level model synthesis.
                    */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /*
                    SR_005 Source Relevance Classification;
                    SR_030 Project Information Isolation;
                    SR_038 Multiple Source Support

                    Added due to ADR-032 Project-Level Multi-Source Reconciliation and Controlled Engineering Evolution
                    */
                }
                attribute :>> scope {
                    doc /* mvp */
                }
                dependency from 'Assess Project Source Admissibility' to
                    '01_Stakeholder'::'01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'03_DataQuality'::'Source Relevance Classification';
                dependency from 'Assess Project Source Admissibility' to
                    '01_Stakeholder'::'01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'02_Architectural'::'Project Information Isolation';
                dependency from 'Assess Project Source Admissibility' to
                    '01_Stakeholder'::'01_Requirements'::'02_StakeholderRequirements'::'01_Functional'::'Multiple Source Support';
            }
            requirement <SYSR_120> 'Enforce Project Source Admissibility' : '00_RequirementDefinitions'::AssuranceSystemRequirement {
                doc /*
                The system shall prevent a source contribution from entering
                project-level model synthesis unless its applicable Project Fit state
                explicitly permits that contribution.
                */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /*
                    Makes Project Fit an effective fail-closed processing gate rather
                    than advisory information and prevents non-admitted source
                    contributions from influencing model synthesis.
                    */
                }
                attribute :>> source {
                    doc /*
                    SR_005 Source Relevance Classification;

                    SR_036 Approved Information Boundary

                    */

            requirement <SYSR_050> 'Support Non-Destructive Model Integration' : '00_RequirementDefinitions'::ArchitectureSystemRequirement {
                doc /* The system shall integrate a generated model unit through defined integration relationships without restructuring existing model elements. */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /* Requires integration through explicit relationships rather than silent restructuring of existing model content. */

                one or more exact source-grounded mentions before professional Persona
                interpretation while preserving all supporting source references and

                */
                attribute :>> rationale {
                    doc /*

                    the same subject and preserves exact source provenance for Human Review.
                    */

                    doc /*

        package '10_DesignConstraints' {
            doc
            /* System Design Constraint modeling note:
             *
             * The constraints in this package document intentionally selected solution-space boundary conditions.
             * Each constraint contains one independently verifiable design obligation.
             * The category packages support navigation only and do not define Logical Components or subsystem boundaries.
             */
            package '00_ConstraintDefinitions' {
                requirement def BaseSystemDesignConstraint {
                    attribute rationale;
                    attribute source;
                    attribute scope;
                    attribute verificationQuestion;
                }
                requirement def SystemDesignConstraint :> BaseSystemDesignConstraint;
                requirement def ArchitectureConstraint :> SystemDesignConstraint {
                    doc /* Constraint related to the structural and orchestration architecture of the Turing Generator. */
                }
                requirement def AgentConstraint :> SystemDesignConstraint {
                    doc /* Constraint related to agent-based candidate generation and agent selection. */
                }
                requirement def RepresentationConstraint :> SystemDesignConstraint {
                    doc /* Constraint related to target notation, artifact structure and validation compatibility. */
                }
                requirement def DataConstraint :> SystemDesignConstraint {
                    doc /* Constraint related to technical identities, operational records and artifact integrity. */
                }
                requirement def UserInterfaceConstraint :> SystemDesignConstraint {
                    doc /* Constraint related to the user interface and its access to system capabilities. */
                }
            }
            package '01_ArchitectureAndOrchestration' {

                    doc /* Machine-readable operational records shall be represented as JSON artifacts. */

                        doc /* Provides deterministic and schema-validatable representations for project and source manifests, processing-state records, decisions and artifact-reference records. */
                    }
                    attribute :>> source {
                        doc /* Former SYSC_013 CanonicalJsonIntermediateRepresentation; Turing Generator implementation principles */
                    }
                    attribute :>> scope {
                        doc /* mvp */
                    }
                    attribute :>> verificationQuestion {
                        doc /* Is every machine-readable operational record represented as a JSON artifact? */
                    }
                    dependency
                            from 'JSON Operational Record Representation'
                            to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Assign Unique Project Identity';

                    dependency
                            from 'JSON Operational Record Representation'

                }
                requirement <SYSC_018> 'Six-Digit Numeric Project Identity' : '00_ConstraintDefinitions'::DataConstraint {
                    doc /* Each project_id shall be an immutable six-character string matching ^[0-9]{6}$. */
                    attribute :>> rationale {
                        doc /* Provides a compact and stable technical identity for project references and project-local storage. */
                    }
                    attribute :>> source {
                        doc /* ADR-005 Project Workspace Architecture; accepted Phase-P identifier decision */
                    }
                    attribute :>> scope {
                        doc /* mvp */
                    }

                    }

                            from 'Six-Digit Numeric Project Identity'
                            to '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Assign Unique Project Identity';
                }
                requirement <SYSC_019> 'Separation of Project Identity and Display Name' : '00_ConstraintDefinitions'::DataConstraint {
                    doc /* A project display name shall not serve as the technical identity of an engineering project. */
                    attribute :>> rationale {
                        doc /* Allows a human-readable project name to change without invalidating technical references. */

                    attribute :>> source {

                    attribute :>> scope {
                        doc /* mvp */

                requirement <SYSC_020> 'SHA-256 Integrity References' : '00_ConstraintDefinitions'::DataConstraint {
                    doc /*
                    Each content-integrity fingerprint used for a retained engineering or
                    processing artifact shall use SHA-256.
                    */
                    attribute :>> rationale {
                        doc /*
                        Provides one consistent content-integrity mechanism across retained
                        processing evidence, engineering-authority artifacts and generated
                        engineering artifacts.
                        */

                    attribute :>> source {

                requirement <SYSC_046> 'Contextual Processing Artifact Identity' : '00_ConstraintDefinitions'::DataConstraint {
                    doc /*
                    Each processing artifact reference used across Processing Runs shall
                    include the originating Processing Run identity in its effective identity.
                    */
                    attribute :>> rationale {
                        doc /*
                        Prevents collisions between valid run-local artifact identifiers when
                        processing artifacts from multiple runs are evaluated within one
                        Project context.
                        */
                    }
                    attribute :>> source {
                        doc /*
                        Added due to ADR-032 Project-Level Multi-Source Reconciliation and Controlled Engineering Evolution
                        */
                    }
                    attribute :>> scope {
                        doc /* mvp */
                    }
                    attribute :>> verificationQuestion {
                        doc /*
                        Can two processing artifacts from different Processing Runs be treated
                        as the same artifact solely because their run-local artifact identities
                        are equal?
                        */
                    }
                    dependency from 'Contextual Processing Artifact Identity' to
                        '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'09_GovernanceSourcesAndHistory'::'Associate Artifacts with Originating Processing Run';
                }
                requirement <SYSC_047> 'Immutable Authority Artifacts' : '00_ConstraintDefinitions'::DataConstraint {
                    doc /*
                    Each finalized artifact that establishes or transfers engineering or
                    model authority shall remain immutable.
                    */
                    attribute :>> rationale {
                        doc /*
                        Ensures that an authority decision remains bound to the exact artifact
                        content that was reviewed, approved or transferred across an authority
                        boundary.
                        */
                    }
                    attribute :>> source {
                        doc /*
                        Added due to ADR-016 Human Review Workspace and Approved Input Promotion Architecture;
                        Added due to ADR-019 Internal Engineering Model Assembly Architecture;
                        Added due to ADR-023 Final Model Review and Output Publication Architecture
                        */
                    }
                    attribute :>> scope {
                        doc /* mvp */
                    }
                    attribute :>> verificationQuestion {
                        doc /*
                        Can a finalized artifact that establishes or transfers engineering or
                        model authority be modified in place?
                        */
                    }
                    dependency from 'Immutable Authority Artifacts' to
                        '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'09_GovernanceSourcesAndHistory'::'Bind Review Decision to Reviewed Content';
                    dependency from 'Immutable Authority Artifacts' to
                        '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'09_GovernanceSourcesAndHistory'::'Require New Review after Content Change';
                    dependency from 'Immutable Authority Artifacts' to
                        '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'09_GovernanceSourcesAndHistory'::'Distinguish Engineering Information Authority State';
                }

                requirement <SYSC_048> 'Non-Authoritative User Interface State' : '00_ConstraintDefinitions'::UserInterfaceConstraint {
                    doc /*
                    User-interface state shall not establish engineering or workflow

                    */
                    attribute :>> rationale {
                        doc /*
                        Prevents transient navigation, display or session state from replacing

                        authority.
                        */

                        doc /* mvp */

                        from user-interface state?

                requirement <SYSC_049> 'Progressive Disclosure of Technical Detail' : '00_ConstraintDefinitions'::UserInterfaceConstraint {
                    doc /*
                    Engineer-facing user-interface views shall use progressive disclosure for
                    technical traceability and processing details.
                    */
                    attribute :>> rationale {
                        doc /*
                        Keeps the default interaction focused on the current engineering task
                        while preserving access to the complete supporting technical evidence.
                        */
                    }

                        */

                    dependency from 'Progressive Disclosure of Technical Detail' to
                        '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'03_AmbiguityReviewAndValidation'::'Explain Generated Engineering Results';
                }
                requirement <SYSC_050> 'Authoritative State Reconstruction after Write' : '00_ConstraintDefinitions'::UserInterfaceConstraint {
                    doc /*
                    After an authoritative user-interface write action, the visible workflow
                    state shall be reconstructed from persisted authoritative evidence.
                    */
                    attribute :>> rationale {
                        doc /*
                        Prevents transient user-interface assumptions from becoming the
                        apparent result of an authoritative engineering action.
                        */
                    }
                    attribute :>> source {
                        doc /*
                        Added due to ADR-024 Guided Engineering Workflow and UX Projection Architecture
                        */
                    }
                    attribute :>> scope {
                        doc /* mvp */
                    }
                    attribute :>> verificationQuestion {
                        doc /*
                        After an authoritative user-interface write action, can the visible
                        workflow state be established without rereading persisted authoritative
                        evidence?
                        */
                    }
                    dependency from 'Authoritative State Reconstruction after Write' to
                        '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Derive Guided Workflow State';
                    dependency from 'Authoritative State Reconstruction after Write' to
                        '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'09_GovernanceSourcesAndHistory'::'Bind Review Decision to Reviewed Content';
                }

        //ENDE//
    }
    package '02_Functional' {
        doc
        /* System Functional modeling note:
         *
         * The System Functions describe the complete intended behavior of the
         * Turing Generator at system level. They cover MVP, target and
         * optional-future System Requirements and are independent of the
         * current implementation status.
         *
         * Each System Requirement has exactly one primary functional allocation.
         * This primary allocation supports readable traceability and later
         * implementation-status assessment. It does not prohibit supporting
         * interactions between System Functions.
         *
         * The System Functions were derived after acceptance of the complete
         * System Requirement and Design Constraint baseline. They shall not be
         * interpreted as Logical Components or subsystem boundaries.
         */
        package '00_FunctionDefinitions' {

            action def <SF_001> 'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context' {
                doc /*
                The system manages persistent engineering projects and their
                project-bound source contexts, including project and source identity,
                source roles, project selection, source registration and source
                selection for engineering processing.
                */

                // Primary System Requirement allocation.
                dependency from 'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Create Engineering Project';
                dependency from 'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Assign Unique Project Identity';
                dependency from 'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Retain Multiple Engineering Projects';
                dependency from 'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Select Active Engineering Project';
                dependency from 'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Reopen Engineering Project';
                dependency from 'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Bind Project-Scoped Information';
                dependency from 'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'07_ProjectAndProcessingTransparency'::'Prevent Cross-Project Information Use';
                dependency from 'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'09_GovernanceSourcesAndHistory'::'Register Engineering Source';

                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'03_AmbiguityReviewAndValidation'::'Prevent Integration of Unresolved Invalid Model Content';

                dependency from 'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'05_ArchitectureConsistency'::'Validate Generated Relationship and Endpoint Consistency';

                dependency from 'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'06_DataCompletionAndArchitectureAsCode'::'Validate SysML v2 Textual Conformance';

                // Directly applicable Design Constraints.
                dependency from 'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'10_DesignConstraints'::'03_RepresentationAndValidation'::'Target-Notation Validation Compatibility';
                dependency from 'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'10_DesignConstraints'::'03_RepresentationAndValidation'::'Target-Artifact Validation Compatibility';
                dependency from 'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'10_DesignConstraints'::'03_RepresentationAndValidation'::'Versioned SysML v2 Validation Profile';
                dependency from 'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'10_DesignConstraints'::'03_RepresentationAndValidation'::'Selected Validation Profile Reference';
                dependency from 'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'10_DesignConstraints'::'03_RepresentationAndValidation'::'Non-Mutating Model Validation';
                dependency from 'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' to
                    '02_System'::'01_Requirements'::'10_DesignConstraints'::'03_RepresentationAndValidation'::'Separate Internal and External Validation';
            }
        }
        package '01_SystemBehavior' {
            private import ScalarValues::*;
            package '00_BehaviorDefinitions' {

                // ============================================================
                // Stable existing behavior definitions.
                // ============================================================

                // Project, source and processing context.
                item def ProjectAndSourceContext;
                item def ProcessingRunContext;
                item def ProcessingAttemptContext;

                // Engineering source and source-local processing.
                item def EngineeringSourceArtifact;
                item def SourceDerivedInformation;
                item def SourceProjection;
                item def PreparedEngineeringInformation;
                item def SourceAnalysisUnitSet;
                item def SourceGroundedEvidenceSet;

                // Semantic processing.
                item def PersonaInterpretation;
                item def ConsolidatedEngineeringSubjectSet;
                item def ConsensusVarianceAssessment;
                item def CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet;
                item def SemanticallyGovernedInformation;

                // Processing and assessment evidence.
                item def DataAssessmentEvidence;
                item def SemanticAssessmentEvidence;
                item def CoverageAssessment;
                item def EngineeringCandidateSet;
                item def CandidateEvidence;

                // Human Engineering Review.
                item def ReviewPackage;
                item def HumanReviewDecision;
                item def ApprovedEngineeringInformation;

                // Project Fit and multi-source admission.
                item def ProjectFitAssessment;
                item def ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;

                // Model derivation and placement.
                item def ModelDerivationContext;
                item def ModelCandidateSet;
                item def ModelPlacementProposalSet;
                item def ApprovedModelPlacementSet;

                // Internal Engineering Model and architecture assurance.
                item def InternalEngineeringModel;
                item def ValidatedInternalEngineeringModel;
                item def ArchitectureValidationFindingSet;

                // SysML v2 generation, validation and release.
                item def GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;
                item def SysMLValidationResult;
                item def PublicationAuthorization;

                // ============================================================
                // Legacy definitions retained temporarily because existing
                // deeper behavior models or external model elements may still
                // reference them by name.
                //
                // Remove only after the complete Functional migration.
                // ============================================================

                item def ArchitectureCandidate;
                item def ValidatedArchitecture;
                item def ValidationFindingSet;
                item def SysMLv2ArtifactSet;
                item def RepresentationFindingSet;

                // Cross-cutting processing evidence and user-facing state.
                item def ProcessingEvidence;
                item def ProcessingHistory;
                item def ProcessingState;
                item def StatusPresentation;
                item def RecoveryDiagnostic;
            }


            package '01_InteractionNetwork' {

                action def <SFB_001> 'System Function Interaction Network' {
                    doc /*
                    Non-sequential interaction model for the System Functions.

                    The flows describe authoritative engineering-information progression,
                    information exchange, Human-review boundaries, processing evidence
                    and workflow-state projection.

                    Source-local processing and Human Engineering Review occur before
                    Project Fit. Only Project-admitted source-local engineering authority
                    inputs may contribute to the unified Model Candidate Set.

                    The flows shall not be interpreted as one mandatory temporal
                    execution sequence.
                    */


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Project and processing context
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action manageContext :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context' {

                        out item projectAndSourceContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                    }


                    action orchestrateRuns :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {

                        in item projectAndSourceContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;

                        out item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        out item processingAttemptContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;

                        out item processingState :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingState;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Source-local processing
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action assessAndPrepare :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {

                        in item projectAndSourceContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item processingAttemptContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;

                        out item sourceProjection :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;

                        out item preparedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PreparedEngineeringInformation;

                        out item assessmentEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::DataAssessmentEvidence;
                    }


                    action applySemantics :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Apply Semantic Governance' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item preparedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PreparedEngineeringInformation;

                        out item canonicalSubjects :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet;

                        out item governedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation;

                        out item semanticEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticAssessmentEvidence;
                    }


                    action generateInterpretationCandidates :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Generate and Compare Engineering Candidates' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item canonicalSubjects :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet;

                        in item governedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation;

                        out item candidateSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringCandidateSet;

                        out item candidateEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CandidateEvidence;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Source-local Human Engineering Review
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action reviewSourceEngineering :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item assessmentEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::DataAssessmentEvidence;

                        in item semanticEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticAssessmentEvidence;

                        in item candidateSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringCandidateSet;

                        in item candidateEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CandidateEvidence;

                        in item processingEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;

                        out item reviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                        out item approvedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Project Fit and multi-source admission
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action assessProjectFit :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {

                        in item projectAndSourceContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item processingAttemptContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;

                        in item sourceProjection :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;

                        in item approvedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;

                        out item projectFitAssessment :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectFitAssessment;
                    }


                    action admitProjectContributions :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {

                        in item projectAndSourceContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;

                        in item projectFitAssessment :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectFitAssessment;

                        in item approvedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;

                        out item admittedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                            ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Coverage and model derivation
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action assessCoverage :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item admittedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                            ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;

                        in item processingEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;

                        out item coverageAssessment :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;
                    }


                    action selectDerivationStrategy :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item admittedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                            ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;

                        out item derivationContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelDerivationContext;
                    }


                    action deriveModelCandidates :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item admittedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                            ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;

                        in item coverageAssessment :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;

                        in item derivationContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelDerivationContext;

                        out item modelCandidateSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Model Candidate Review and Model Placement
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action reviewModelCandidates :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item modelCandidateSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;

                        in item processingEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;

                        out item reviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                    }


                    action deriveModelPlacement :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item modelCandidateSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;

                        in item candidateReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                        out item placementProposals :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelPlacementProposalSet;
                    }


                    action reviewModelPlacement :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item placementProposals :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelPlacementProposalSet;

                        in item processingEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;

                        out item placementReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                        out item approvedPlacementSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedModelPlacementSet;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Deterministic Model Assembly and architecture assurance
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action assembleInternalModel :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item admittedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                            ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;

                        in item modelCandidateSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;

                        in item approvedPlacementSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedModelPlacementSet;

                        out item internalEngineeringModel :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel;
                    }


                    action validateArchitecture :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Validate and Integrate Architecture Content' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item internalEngineeringModel :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel;

                        out item architectureFindings :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ArchitectureValidationFindingSet;

                        out item validatedInternalModel :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                            ValidatedInternalEngineeringModel;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // SysML v2 generation and validation
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action generateSysML :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Generate SysML v2 Architecture Artifacts' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item validatedInternalModel :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                            ValidatedInternalEngineeringModel;

                        out item generatedArtifactSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;
                    }


                    action validateSysML :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item generatedArtifactSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;

                        out item validationResult :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Final Human Model Review and publication authority
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action finalModelReview :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item generatedArtifactSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;

                        in item validationResult :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;

                        in item processingEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;

                        out item finalReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                    }


                    action publicationGate :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {

                        in item generatedArtifactSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;

                        in item validationResult :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;

                        in item finalReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                        out item publicationAuthorization :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Cross-cutting evidence and traceability
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action maintainEvidence :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
