                        'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability' {

                        in item projectAndSourceContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item processingAttemptContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;

                        in item sourceProjection :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;

                        in item assessmentEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::DataAssessmentEvidence;

                        in item semanticEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticAssessmentEvidence;

                        in item candidateEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CandidateEvidence;

                        in item approvedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;

                        in item projectFitAssessment :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectFitAssessment;

                        in item coverageAssessment :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;

                        in item modelCandidateSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;

                        in item approvedPlacementSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedModelPlacementSet;

                        in item internalEngineeringModel :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel;

                        in item architectureFindings :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ArchitectureValidationFindingSet;

                        in item generatedArtifactSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;

                        in item validationResult :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;

                        in item sourceReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                        in item modelCandidateReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                        in item placementReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                        in item finalReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                        in item publicationAuthorization :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization;

                        out item processingEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;

                        out item processingHistory :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingHistory;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Engineer-facing workflow projection
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action presentStatus :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Present Processing Status and History' {

                        in item projectAndSourceContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;

                        in item processingState :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingState;

                        in item coverageAssessment :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;

                        in item processingEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingEvidence;

                        in item processingHistory :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingHistory;

                        in item sourceReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                        in item modelCandidateReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                        in item placementReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                        in item finalReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                        in item validationResult :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;

                        in item publicationAuthorization :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization;

                        out item statusPresentation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::StatusPresentation;
                    }


                    // ============================================================
                    // FLOWS
                    // ============================================================


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Project and processing context
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow from manageContext.projectAndSourceContext
                        to orchestrateRuns.projectAndSourceContext;

                    flow from manageContext.projectAndSourceContext
                        to assessAndPrepare.projectAndSourceContext;

                    flow from manageContext.projectAndSourceContext
                        to assessProjectFit.projectAndSourceContext;

                    flow from manageContext.projectAndSourceContext
                        to admitProjectContributions.projectAndSourceContext;

                    flow from manageContext.projectAndSourceContext
                        to maintainEvidence.projectAndSourceContext;

                    flow from manageContext.projectAndSourceContext
                        to presentStatus.projectAndSourceContext;


                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to assessAndPrepare.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to applySemantics.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to generateInterpretationCandidates.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to reviewSourceEngineering.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to assessProjectFit.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to assessCoverage.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to selectDerivationStrategy.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to deriveModelCandidates.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to reviewModelCandidates.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to deriveModelPlacement.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to reviewModelPlacement.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to assembleInternalModel.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to validateArchitecture.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to generateSysML.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to validateSysML.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to finalModelReview.processingRunContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingRunContext
                        to maintainEvidence.processingRunContext;


                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingAttemptContext
                        to assessAndPrepare.processingAttemptContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingAttemptContext
                        to assessProjectFit.processingAttemptContext;

                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingAttemptContext
                        to maintainEvidence.processingAttemptContext;


                    flow from orchestrateRuns.processingState
                        to presentStatus.processingState;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Source-local progression
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow from assessAndPrepare.preparedInformation
                        to applySemantics.preparedInformation;

                    flow from applySemantics.canonicalSubjects
                        to generateInterpretationCandidates.canonicalSubjects;

                    flow from applySemantics.governedInformation
                        to generateInterpretationCandidates.governedInformation;

                    flow from assessAndPrepare.assessmentEvidence
                        to reviewSourceEngineering.assessmentEvidence;

                    flow from applySemantics.semanticEvidence
                        to reviewSourceEngineering.semanticEvidence;

                    flow from generateInterpretationCandidates.candidateSet
                        to reviewSourceEngineering.candidateSet;

                    flow from generateInterpretationCandidates.candidateEvidence
                        to reviewSourceEngineering.candidateEvidence;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Project Fit and source admission
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow from assessAndPrepare.sourceProjection
                        to assessProjectFit.sourceProjection;

                    flow from reviewSourceEngineering.approvedInformation
                        to assessProjectFit.approvedInformation;

                    flow from assessProjectFit.projectFitAssessment
                        to admitProjectContributions.projectFitAssessment;

                    flow from reviewSourceEngineering.approvedInformation
                        to admitProjectContributions.approvedInformation;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Coverage and unified Model Candidate derivation
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow from admitProjectContributions.admittedInformation
                        to assessCoverage.admittedInformation;

                    flow from admitProjectContributions.admittedInformation
                        to selectDerivationStrategy.admittedInformation;

                    flow from admitProjectContributions.admittedInformation
                        to deriveModelCandidates.admittedInformation;

                    flow from assessCoverage.coverageAssessment
                        to deriveModelCandidates.coverageAssessment;

                    flow from selectDerivationStrategy.derivationContext
                        to deriveModelCandidates.derivationContext;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Candidate review, placement and model assembly
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow from deriveModelCandidates.modelCandidateSet
                        to reviewModelCandidates.modelCandidateSet;

                    flow from deriveModelCandidates.modelCandidateSet
                        to deriveModelPlacement.modelCandidateSet;

                    flow from reviewModelCandidates.reviewDecision
                        to deriveModelPlacement.candidateReviewDecision;

                    flow from deriveModelPlacement.placementProposals
                        to reviewModelPlacement.placementProposals;

                    flow from deriveModelCandidates.modelCandidateSet
                        to assembleInternalModel.modelCandidateSet;

                    flow from reviewModelPlacement.approvedPlacementSet
                        to assembleInternalModel.approvedPlacementSet;

                    flow from admitProjectContributions.admittedInformation
                        to assembleInternalModel.admittedInformation;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // IEM, SysML generation and validation
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow from assembleInternalModel.internalEngineeringModel
                        to validateArchitecture.internalEngineeringModel;

                    flow from validateArchitecture.validatedInternalModel
                        to generateSysML.validatedInternalModel;

                    flow from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet
                        to validateSysML.generatedArtifactSet;

                    flow from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet
                        to finalModelReview.generatedArtifactSet;

                    flow from validateSysML.validationResult
                        to finalModelReview.validationResult;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Final release authority
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet
                        to publicationGate.generatedArtifactSet;

                    flow from validateSysML.validationResult
                        to publicationGate.validationResult;

                    flow from finalModelReview.finalReviewDecision
                        to publicationGate.finalReviewDecision;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Evidence collection
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow from assessAndPrepare.sourceProjection
                        to maintainEvidence.sourceProjection;

                    flow from assessAndPrepare.assessmentEvidence
                        to maintainEvidence.assessmentEvidence;

                    flow from applySemantics.semanticEvidence
                        to maintainEvidence.semanticEvidence;

                    flow from generateInterpretationCandidates.candidateEvidence
                        to maintainEvidence.candidateEvidence;

                    flow from reviewSourceEngineering.approvedInformation
                        to maintainEvidence.approvedInformation;

                    flow from assessProjectFit.projectFitAssessment
                        to maintainEvidence.projectFitAssessment;

                    flow from assessCoverage.coverageAssessment
                        to maintainEvidence.coverageAssessment;

                    flow from deriveModelCandidates.modelCandidateSet
                        to maintainEvidence.modelCandidateSet;

                    flow from reviewModelPlacement.approvedPlacementSet
                        to maintainEvidence.approvedPlacementSet;

                    flow from assembleInternalModel.internalEngineeringModel
                        to maintainEvidence.internalEngineeringModel;

                    flow from validateArchitecture.architectureFindings
                        to maintainEvidence.architectureFindings;

                    flow from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet
                        to maintainEvidence.generatedArtifactSet;

                    flow from validateSysML.validationResult
                        to maintainEvidence.validationResult;

                    flow from reviewSourceEngineering.reviewDecision
                        to maintainEvidence.sourceReviewDecision;

                    flow from reviewModelCandidates.reviewDecision
                        to maintainEvidence.modelCandidateReviewDecision;

                    flow from reviewModelPlacement.placementReviewDecision
                        to maintainEvidence.placementReviewDecision;

                    flow from finalModelReview.finalReviewDecision
                        to maintainEvidence.finalReviewDecision;

                    flow from publicationGate.publicationAuthorization
                        to maintainEvidence.publicationAuthorization;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Persisted evidence feeds review, coverage and workflow views
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingEvidence
                        to reviewSourceEngineering.processingEvidence;

                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingEvidence
                        to assessCoverage.processingEvidence;

                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingEvidence
                        to reviewModelCandidates.processingEvidence;

                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingEvidence
                        to reviewModelPlacement.processingEvidence;

                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingEvidence
                        to finalModelReview.processingEvidence;

                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingEvidence
                        to presentStatus.processingEvidence;

                    flow from maintainEvidence.processingHistory
                        to presentStatus.processingHistory;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Engineer-facing status projection
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow from assessCoverage.coverageAssessment
                        to presentStatus.coverageAssessment;

                    flow from reviewSourceEngineering.reviewDecision
                        to presentStatus.sourceReviewDecision;

                    flow from reviewModelCandidates.reviewDecision
                        to presentStatus.modelCandidateReviewDecision;

                    flow from reviewModelPlacement.placementReviewDecision
                        to presentStatus.placementReviewDecision;

                    flow from finalModelReview.finalReviewDecision
                        to presentStatus.finalReviewDecision;

                    flow from validateSysML.validationResult
                        to presentStatus.validationResult;

                    flow from publicationGate.publicationAuthorization
                        to presentStatus.publicationAuthorization;
                }
            }
            package '02_EngineeringLifecycle' {

                action def <SFB_002> 'Engineering Transformation Lifecycle' {

                    doc /*
                    Control-oriented lifecycle for transformation of source-grounded
                    engineering information into a validated and Human-authorized
                    SysML v2 artifact set.

                    Source-local processing and Human Engineering Review establish
                    source-local engineering authority before Project Fit is assessed.

                    Only Project-admitted source-local authority inputs may contribute
                    to Project-level model synthesis.

                    Human Model Placement Review precedes deterministic model assembly.
                    SysML v2 generation and SysML v2 validation are separate lifecycle
                    stages.

                    Final publication requires both successful mandatory validation
                    and explicit Human approval of the exact generated artifact revision.

                    Rework that changes previously approved engineering information
                    returns to the applicable upstream Human-review boundary.
                    */


                    // ============================================================
                    // Project and Processing Run establishment
                    // ============================================================

                    first start;

                    then action manageContext :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context';

                    then action configureRun :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';


                    // ============================================================
                    // Source-local input assessment
                    // ============================================================

                    then merge sourceProcessingEntry;

                    then action assessInput :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {

                        out assessmentReady : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide assessmentGate;

                    if assessInput.assessmentReady then semanticEntry;
                    else reviewInput;


                    action reviewInput :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        out acceptInput : Boolean;
                        out requestInputChanges : Boolean;
                        out rejectInput : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide inputReviewGate;

                    if reviewInput.requestInputChanges then sourceProcessingEntry;
                    if reviewInput.acceptInput then semanticEntry;
                    if reviewInput.rejectInput then stopRun;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Source-grounded semantic processing
                    // ============================================================

                    merge semanticEntry;

                    then action applySemanticGovernance :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Apply Semantic Governance' {

                        out semanticReady : Boolean;
                        out semanticResolutionRequired : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide semanticGate;

                    if applySemanticGovernance.semanticReady then
                        sourceCoverageEntry;

                    if applySemanticGovernance.semanticResolutionRequired then
                        generateSemanticCandidates;


                    action generateSemanticCandidates :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Generate and Compare Engineering Candidates';


                    then action reviewSemanticResolution :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        out semanticResolved : Boolean;
                        out requestSemanticChanges : Boolean;
                        out rejectSemanticResolution : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide semanticReviewGate;

                    if reviewSemanticResolution.semanticResolved then semanticEntry;

                    if reviewSemanticResolution.requestSemanticChanges then
                        generateSemanticCandidates;

                    if reviewSemanticResolution.rejectSemanticResolution then
                        stopRun;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Source-local evidence sufficiency
                    // ============================================================

                    merge sourceCoverageEntry;

                    then action assessSourceEvidenceCoverage :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency' {

                        out evidenceSufficient : Boolean;
                        out evidenceDeficiencyRequiresReview : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide sourceCoverageGate;

                    if assessSourceEvidenceCoverage.evidenceSufficient then
                        sourceReviewEntry;

                    if assessSourceEvidenceCoverage.evidenceDeficiencyRequiresReview then
                        reviewEvidenceDeficiency;


                    action reviewEvidenceDeficiency :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        out requestAdditionalInformation : Boolean;
                        out requestAlternativeInterpretation : Boolean;
                        out acceptKnownEvidenceGap : Boolean;
                        out rejectForInsufficientEvidence : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide evidenceReviewGate;

                    if reviewEvidenceDeficiency.requestAdditionalInformation then
                        sourceProcessingEntry;

                    if reviewEvidenceDeficiency.requestAlternativeInterpretation then
                        semanticEntry;

                    if reviewEvidenceDeficiency.acceptKnownEvidenceGap then
                        sourceReviewEntry;

                    if reviewEvidenceDeficiency.rejectForInsufficientEvidence then
                        stopRun;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Source-local Human Engineering Review
                    // ============================================================

                    merge sourceReviewEntry;

                    then action reviewSourceEngineering :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        out approveSourceInformation : Boolean;
                        out requestSourceChanges : Boolean;
                        out rejectSourceInformation : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide sourceReviewGate;

                    if reviewSourceEngineering.approveSourceInformation then
                        projectFitEntry;

                    if reviewSourceEngineering.requestSourceChanges then
                        sourceProcessingEntry;

                    if reviewSourceEngineering.rejectSourceInformation then
                        stopRun;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Project Fit
                    // ============================================================

                    merge projectFitEntry;

                    then action assessProjectFit :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {

                        out projectFitAdmitted : Boolean;
                        out projectFitReassessmentRequired : Boolean;
                        out projectFitRejected : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide projectFitGate;

                    if assessProjectFit.projectFitAdmitted then
                        enforceProjectFit;

                    if assessProjectFit.projectFitReassessmentRequired then
                        sourceProcessingEntry;

                    if assessProjectFit.projectFitRejected then
                        completeWithoutProjectContribution;


                    action enforceProjectFit :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';

                    then projectSynthesisEntry;


                    action completeWithoutProjectContribution :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';

                    then done;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Project-level admitted source population
                    // ============================================================

                    merge projectSynthesisEntry;

                    then action prepareProjectSynthesisInputs :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';


                    // ============================================================
                    // Project-level target-projection coverage
                    // ============================================================

                    then merge targetCoverageEntry;

                    then action assessTargetProjectionCoverage :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency' {

                        out targetProjectionReady : Boolean;
                        out unresolvedProjectionRetained : Boolean;
                        out sourceEvidenceReworkRequired : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide targetCoverageGate;

                    if assessTargetProjectionCoverage.targetProjectionReady then
                        derivationStrategyEntry;

                    if assessTargetProjectionCoverage.unresolvedProjectionRetained then
                        derivationStrategyEntry;

                    if assessTargetProjectionCoverage.sourceEvidenceReworkRequired then
                        sourceProcessingEntry;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Model derivation strategy
                    // ============================================================

                    merge derivationStrategyEntry;

                    then action selectModelDerivationStrategy :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';

                    then modelCandidateEntry;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Unified Model Candidate Set
                    // ============================================================

                    merge modelCandidateEntry;

                    then action deriveModelCandidates :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';

                    then action reviewModelCandidates :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        out approveModelCandidateSet : Boolean;
                        out requestCandidateChanges : Boolean;
                        out rejectModelCandidateSet : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide modelCandidateReviewGate;

                    if reviewModelCandidates.approveModelCandidateSet then
                        modelPlacementEntry;

                    if reviewModelCandidates.requestCandidateChanges then
                        modelCandidateEntry;

                    if reviewModelCandidates.rejectModelCandidateSet then
                        assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Review-driven alternative derivation strategy
                    // ============================================================

                    action assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {

                        out alternativeStrategyAvailable : Boolean;
                        out noAlternativeStrategyAvailable : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide alternativeStrategyGate;

                    if assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy.alternativeStrategyAvailable then
                        selectAlternativeDerivationStrategy;

                    if assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy.noAlternativeStrategyAvailable then
                        stopRun;


                    action selectAlternativeDerivationStrategy :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';

                    then modelCandidateEntry;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Model Placement
                    // ============================================================

                    merge modelPlacementEntry;

                    then action deriveModelPlacement :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';

                    then action reviewModelPlacement :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        out approveModelPlacement : Boolean;
                        out requestPlacementChanges : Boolean;
                        out rejectModelPlacement : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide modelPlacementReviewGate;

                    if reviewModelPlacement.approveModelPlacement then
                        modelAssemblyEntry;

                    if reviewModelPlacement.requestPlacementChanges then
                        modelPlacementEntry;

                    if reviewModelPlacement.rejectModelPlacement then
                        stopRun;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Deterministic Model Assembly
                    // ============================================================

                    merge modelAssemblyEntry;

                    then action assembleInternalEngineeringModel :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';


                    // ============================================================
                    // Architecture-level assurance of the IEM
                    // ============================================================

                    then action validateInternalEngineeringModel :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Validate and Integrate Architecture Content' {

                        out architectureAccepted : Boolean;
                        out modelReworkRequired : Boolean;
                        out placementReworkRequired : Boolean;
                        out upstreamAuthorityReworkRequired : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide internalModelValidationGate;

                    if validateInternalEngineeringModel.architectureAccepted then
                        artifactGenerationEntry;

                    if validateInternalEngineeringModel.modelReworkRequired then
                        modelCandidateEntry;

                    if validateInternalEngineeringModel.placementReworkRequired then
                        modelPlacementEntry;

                    if validateInternalEngineeringModel.upstreamAuthorityReworkRequired then
                        sourceProcessingEntry;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Deterministic SysML v2 Generation
                    // ============================================================

                    merge artifactGenerationEntry;

                    then action generateSysMLArtifacts :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Generate SysML v2 Architecture Artifacts' {

                        out generationSuccessful : Boolean;
                        out serializationCorrectionRequired : Boolean;
                        out internalModelChangeRequired : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide generationGate;

                    if generateSysMLArtifacts.generationSuccessful then
                        artifactValidationEntry;

                    if generateSysMLArtifacts.serializationCorrectionRequired then
                        artifactGenerationEntry;

                    if generateSysMLArtifacts.internalModelChangeRequired then
                        modelCandidateEntry;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Separate SysML v2 Validation
                    // ============================================================

                    merge artifactValidationEntry;

                    then action validateGeneratedSysML :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' {

                        out validationPassed : Boolean;
                        out serializationCorrectionRequired : Boolean;
                        out internalModelChangeRequired : Boolean;
                        out unrecoverableValidationFailure : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide sysmlValidationGate;

                    if validateGeneratedSysML.validationPassed then
                        finalReviewEntry;

                    if validateGeneratedSysML.serializationCorrectionRequired then
                        artifactGenerationEntry;

                    if validateGeneratedSysML.internalModelChangeRequired then
                        modelCandidateEntry;

                    if validateGeneratedSysML.unrecoverableValidationFailure then
                        stopRun;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Final Human Model Review
                    // ============================================================

                    merge finalReviewEntry;

                    then action finalModelReview :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        out approvePublication : Boolean;
                        out modelChangeRequired : Boolean;
                        out serializationChangeRequired : Boolean;
                        out rejectPublication : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide finalReviewGate;

                    if finalModelReview.approvePublication then
                        publicationGateEntry;

                    if finalModelReview.modelChangeRequired then
                        modelCandidateEntry;

                    if finalModelReview.serializationChangeRequired then
                        artifactGenerationEntry;

                    if finalModelReview.rejectPublication then
                        stopRun;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Final Publication Gate
                    // ============================================================

                    merge publicationGateEntry;

                    then action enforcePublicationGate :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {

                        out publicationAuthorized : Boolean;
                        out publicationBlocked : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide publicationGate;

                    if enforcePublicationGate.publicationAuthorized then
                        recordPublication;

                    if enforcePublicationGate.publicationBlocked then
                        stopRun;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Publication evidence and successful completion
                    // ============================================================

                    action recordPublication :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';

                    then action completeRun :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';

                    then done;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Fail-closed termination
                    // ============================================================

                    action stopRun :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';

                    then done;
                }
            }
            package '03_ReviewRecoveryAndSupersession' {

                action def <SFB_003> 'Review Recovery and Supersession' {

                    doc /*
                    Operational control behavior for technical retry, review-driven rework,
                    alternative model derivation, interrupted publication recovery and
                    material-change supersession.

                    A Processing Run may continue through a new Processing Attempt only
                    when its authoritative run bindings remain unchanged.

                    A material binding change requires an explicit successor Processing Run.

                    Rejection of a Model Candidate Set may trigger an alternative permitted
                    derivation strategy before model synthesis is abandoned.

                    Publication recovery preserves the exact generated artifact, validation
                    and Human-release authority boundaries.

                    Earlier processing evidence remains retained and traceable.
                    */


                    // ============================================================
                    // Classify continuation condition
                    // ============================================================

                    first start;

                    then action classifyContinuationCondition :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {

                        out normalContinuation : Boolean;
                        out technicalFailure : Boolean;
                        out interruptedPublication : Boolean;
                        out reviewChangesRequested : Boolean;
                        out modelCandidateRejected : Boolean;
                        out otherReviewedTargetRejected : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide continuationConditionGate;

                    if classifyContinuationCondition.normalContinuation then
                        resumeStage;

                    if classifyContinuationCondition.technicalFailure then
                        recordTechnicalFailure;

                    if classifyContinuationCondition.interruptedPublication then
                        assessPublicationRecoveryState;

                    if classifyContinuationCondition.reviewChangesRequested then
                        assessChangeMateriality;

                    if classifyContinuationCondition.modelCandidateRejected then
                        assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy;

                    if classifyContinuationCondition.otherReviewedTargetRejected then
                        recordReviewRejection;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Technical retry
                    // ============================================================

                    action recordTechnicalFailure :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';

                    then action assessRetryCompatibility :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {

                        out bindingsUnchanged : Boolean;
                        out bindingsChanged : Boolean;
                        out retryNotPermitted : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide retryCompatibilityGate;

                    if assessRetryCompatibility.bindingsUnchanged then
                        createNewAttempt;

                    if assessRetryCompatibility.bindingsChanged then
                        createSuccessorRun;

                    if assessRetryCompatibility.retryNotPermitted then
                        blockRun;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Human-requested changes
                    // ============================================================

                    action assessChangeMateriality :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {

                        out bindingsUnchanged : Boolean;
                        out bindingsChanged : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide changeMaterialityGate;

                    if assessChangeMateriality.bindingsUnchanged then
                        createNewAttempt;

                    if assessChangeMateriality.bindingsChanged then
                        createSuccessorRun;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Review-driven alternative model derivation
                    // ============================================================

                    action assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {

                        out alternativeStrategyAvailable : Boolean;
                        out noAlternativeStrategyAvailable : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide alternativeDerivationGate;

                    if assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy.alternativeStrategyAvailable then
                        selectAlternativeDerivationStrategy;

                    if assessAlternativeDerivationStrategy.noAlternativeStrategyAvailable then
                        recordReviewRejection;


                    action selectAlternativeDerivationStrategy :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';

                    then action recordDerivationStrategyChange :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';

                    then action assessAlternativeStrategyCompatibility :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {

                        out bindingsUnchanged : Boolean;
                        out bindingsChanged : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide alternativeStrategyCompatibilityGate;

                    if assessAlternativeStrategyCompatibility.bindingsUnchanged then
                        createNewAttempt;

                    if assessAlternativeStrategyCompatibility.bindingsChanged then
                        createSuccessorRun;


                    // ============================================================
                    // New attempt within current Processing Run
                    // ============================================================

                    action createNewAttempt :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';

                    then action retainAttemptEvidence :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';

                    then resumeStage;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Successor Processing Run
                    // ============================================================

                    action createSuccessorRun :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';

                    then action retainPredecessorEvidence :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';

                    then action appendSupersessionEvent :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';

                    then resumeStage;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Interrupted publication recovery
                    // ============================================================

                    action assessPublicationRecoveryState :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability' {

                        out publicationAlreadyComplete : Boolean;
                        out publicationCanResume : Boolean;
                        out publicationHistoryInconsistent : Boolean;
                    }

                    then decide publicationRecoveryGate;

                    if assessPublicationRecoveryState.publicationAlreadyComplete then
                        completePublicationHistory;

                    if assessPublicationRecoveryState.publicationCanResume then
                        resumePublicationGate;

                    if assessPublicationRecoveryState.publicationHistoryInconsistent then
                        blockRun;


                    action completePublicationHistory :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';

                    then resumeStage;


                    action resumePublicationGate :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';

                    then done;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Non-recoverable Human rejection
                    // ============================================================

                    action recordReviewRejection :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval';

                    then action retainReviewRejectionEvidence :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';

                    then action completeWithoutPublication :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';

                    then done;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Fail-closed blocked state
                    // ============================================================

                    action blockRun :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';

                    then action presentRecoveryAttention :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Present Processing Status and History';

                    then done;


                    // ============================================================
                    // Handoff back to applicable lifecycle stage
                    // ============================================================

                    action resumeStage :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';

                    then done;
                }
            }
            package '04_EngineeringDataFlow' {

                package '00_ProcessingContextDefinitions' {

                    part def 'LLM Inference Service' {
                        doc /*
                        External inference capability used by selected processing steps.
                        LLM output is non-authoritative processing evidence.
                        The service has no engineering approval authority.
                        */
                    }
                }
                // ================================================================
                // SFB_005
                // Detailed source-local information processing
                // ================================================================
                action def <SFB_005> 'Engineering Information Processing Flow' {
                    doc /*
                    Detailed source-local processing behavior from a registered
                    Engineering Source to the Human Engineering Review boundary.
                    Source Projection and Source Analysis Unit identity are established
                    before source-grounded Evidence Detection.
                    Canonical Engineering Subjects are established before Persona
                    Interpretation so that all configured Personas interpret the same
                    source-grounded engineering subjects.
                    Persona Interpretation, consensus and variance assessment, controlled
                    semantic governance and evidence-coverage assessment prepare the
                    persisted Human Engineering Review package.
                    Project Fit and Project-level model derivation are outside this
                    source-local behavior and occur only after explicit Human Engineering
                    approval.
                    */
                    in item engineeringSource :
                        '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringSourceArtifact;
                    in item projectAndSourceContext :
                        '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                    in item processingRunContext :
                        '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                    // Source Projection is also retained for the later Project Fit step.
                    out item sourceProjection :
                        '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;
                    out item reviewPackage :
                        '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ReviewPackage;


                    ref part llmService :
                        '00_ProcessingContextDefinitions'::'LLM Inference Service';
                    ref part humanReviewer :
                        '01_Stakeholder'::'00_Stakeholders'::HumanRole {

                        doc /*
                        External Human engineering authority at the Human Engineering Review
                        boundary.

                        The Human Reviewer evaluates the persisted Review Package. Automated
                        processing and LLM-supported interpretation do not substitute this
                        authority.
                        */
                    }

                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Reusable Persona interpretation behavior
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action def PersonaCanonicalInterpretation {

                        doc /*
                        One execution represents one configured Persona interpreting
                        the same persisted Canonical Engineering Subject population
                        and its source-grounded evidence.

                        Repeated executions of the same Persona remain separate
                        processing observations and do not constitute additional
                        Persona votes.
                        */

                        in item sourceEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet;

                        in item canonicalSubjects :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet;

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        out item interpretation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation;
                    }


                    // ============================================================
                    // Nominal source-local processing sequence
                    // ============================================================

                    first start;
                    then P01;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // P01 - deterministic source projection
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action <P01> 'Create Deterministic Source Projection' :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {

                        in item engineeringSource :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringSourceArtifact;

                        in item projectAndSourceContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;

                        out item sourceProjection :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // P02 - deterministic analysis units
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action <P02> 'Establish Source Analysis Units' :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {

                        in item sourceProjection :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        out item sourceAnalysisUnits :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceAnalysisUnitSet;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // P03 - source-grounded evidence detection
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action <P03> 'Detect Source-Grounded Evidence' :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {

                        in item sourceAnalysisUnits :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceAnalysisUnitSet;

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        out item sourceEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // P04 - canonical engineering subjects
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action <P04> 'Discover Canonical Engineering Subjects' :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Apply Semantic Governance' {

                        in item sourceEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet;

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        out item canonicalSubjects :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // P05 - common-subject Persona interpretation
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    action <P05> 'Interpret Canonical Subjects with Configured Personas' :
                        PersonaCanonicalInterpretation [1..*];
                    // P05 is intentionally replicated for the configured Persona set.
                    first P01 then P02;
                    first P02 then P03;
                    first P03 then P04;
                    first P04 then P05;
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Persona multiplicity / consensus
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    action assessPersonaMultiplicity :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Generate and Compare Engineering Candidates' {

                        in item personaInterpretations :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation [*];

                        out hasMultiplePersonas : Boolean;
                    }
                    first P05 then assessPersonaMultiplicity;

                    then decide personaMultiplicityGate;

                    // The decision uses the persisted interpretation population.
                    if assessPersonaMultiplicity.hasMultiplePersonas then P06;
                    else personaAssessmentComplete;
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // P06 - consensus and variance
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    action <P06> 'Assess Persona Consensus and Variance' :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Generate and Compare Engineering Candidates' {

                        in item personaInterpretations :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation [*];

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        out item consensusVariance :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ConsensusVarianceAssessment;
                    }
                    then personaAssessmentComplete;
                    merge personaAssessmentComplete;
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // P07 - controlled semantic governance / alignment
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    then P07;
                    action <P07> 'Apply Controlled Semantic Governance' :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Apply Semantic Governance' {

                        in item sourceEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet;

                        in item canonicalSubjects :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet;

                        in item personaInterpretations :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation [*];

                        in item consensusVariance :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ConsensusVarianceAssessment [0..1];

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        out item governedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation;

                        out item semanticEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticAssessmentEvidence;
                    }
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // P08 - evidence / coverage assessment
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    then P08;

                    action <P08> 'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency' :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency' {

                        in item governedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation;

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        out item coverageAssessment :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;
                    }
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // P09 - Human Engineering Review package
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    then P09;
                    action <P09> 'Prepare Human Review Package' :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        in item canonicalSubjects :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet;
                        in item governedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation;
                        in item semanticEvidence :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticAssessmentEvidence;
                        in item consensusVariance :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ConsensusVarianceAssessment [0..1];
                        in item coverageAssessment :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;
                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;
                        out item reviewPackage :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ReviewPackage;
                    }

                    then done;
                    // ============================================================
                    // Bindings
                    // ============================================================

                    binding bind P01.engineeringSource = engineeringSource;
                    binding bind P01.projectAndSourceContext = projectAndSourceContext;
                    binding bind P01.sourceProjection = sourceProjection;
                    binding bind P02.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P03.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P04.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P05.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P06.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P07.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P08.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P09.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                    binding bind P09.reviewPackage = reviewPackage;
                    // ============================================================
                    // Information flows
                    // ============================================================
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection
                        from P01.sourceProjection
                        to P02.sourceProjection;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceAnalysisUnitSet
                        from P02.sourceAnalysisUnits
                        to P03.sourceAnalysisUnits;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet
                        from P03.sourceEvidence
                        to P04.sourceEvidence;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet
                        from P03.sourceEvidence
                        to P05.sourceEvidence;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet
                        from P04.canonicalSubjects
                        to P05.canonicalSubjects;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation
                        from P05.interpretation
                        to assessPersonaMultiplicity.personaInterpretations;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation
                        from P05.interpretation
                        to P06.personaInterpretations;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PersonaInterpretation
                        from P05.interpretation
                        to P07.personaInterpretations;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceGroundedEvidenceSet
                        from P03.sourceEvidence
                        to P07.sourceEvidence;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet
                        from P04.canonicalSubjects
                        to P07.canonicalSubjects;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CanonicalEngineeringSubjectSet
                        from P04.canonicalSubjects
                        to P09.canonicalSubjects;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ConsensusVarianceAssessment
                        from P06.consensusVariance
                        to P07.consensusVariance;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ConsensusVarianceAssessment
                        from P06.consensusVariance
                        to P09.consensusVariance;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation
                        from P07.governedInformation
                        to P08.governedInformation;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticallyGovernedInformation
                        from P07.governedInformation
                        to P09.governedInformation;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SemanticAssessmentEvidence
                        from P07.semanticEvidence
                        to P09.semanticEvidence;
                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment
                        from P08.coverageAssessment
                        to P09.coverageAssessment;
                    // Selected LLM-backed processing steps.
                    dependency from P03 to llmService;
                    dependency from P05 to llmService;
                    dependency from P07 to llmService;
                    dependency from P09 to humanReviewer;
                }
                // ================================================================
                // SFB_004
                // Presentation-oriented nominal end-to-end data transformation
                // ================================================================

                action def <SFB_004> 'Engineering Data Transformation Flow' {

                    doc /*
                    Data-oriented overview of the nominal transformation of registered
                    Engineering Source information into published SysML v2 artifacts.

                    The behavior intentionally remains compact for engineering
                    communication and presentation.

                    Complex authority and model-synthesis stages are represented by
                    collapsed actions. Their detailed gates, review decisions, rework
                    and recovery behavior are modeled in SFB_001, SFB_002 and SFB_003.

                    The overview therefore shows the path of engineering data through
                    the system without reproducing the complete control lifecycle.
                    */


                    ref part humanReviewer :
                        '01_Stakeholder'::'00_Stakeholders'::HumanRole;

                    ref part llmService :
                        '00_ProcessingContextDefinitions'::'LLM Inference Service';


                    // ============================================================
                    // Collapsed Project Fit behavior
                    // ============================================================

                    action def ProjectFitAdmission {

                        doc /*
                        Compact representation of Project Fit assessment and enforcement.

                        The behavior admits source-local Approved Engineering Information
                        only when its exact source projection and processing context satisfy
                        the applicable Project Fit conditions.
                        */


                        in item sourceProjection :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;

                        in item approvedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;

                        in item projectAndSourceContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        in item processingAttemptContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;

                        out item admittedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                            ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;


                        action assessFit :
                            '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                            'Assess and Prepare Source Information' {

                            in item sourceProjection :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection;

                            in item approvedInformation :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;

                            in item projectAndSourceContext :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;

                            in item processingRunContext :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                            in item processingAttemptContext :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;

                            out item projectFitAssessment :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectFitAssessment;
                        }


                        action enforceFit :
                            '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                            'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {

                            in item projectFitAssessment :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectFitAssessment;

                            in item approvedInformation :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;

                            out item admittedInformation :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                                ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;
                        }


                        first assessFit then enforceFit;


                        binding bind assessFit.sourceProjection = sourceProjection;
                        binding bind assessFit.approvedInformation = approvedInformation;
