                        binding bind assessFit.projectAndSourceContext = projectAndSourceContext;
                        binding bind assessFit.processingRunContext = processingRunContext;
                        binding bind assessFit.processingAttemptContext = processingAttemptContext;

                        binding bind enforceFit.approvedInformation = approvedInformation;
                        binding bind enforceFit.admittedInformation = admittedInformation;


                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectFitAssessment
                            from assessFit.projectFitAssessment
                            to enforceFit.projectFitAssessment;
                    }


                    // ============================================================
                    // Collapsed Project-level model synthesis behavior
                    // ============================================================

                    action def ProjectModelSynthesis {

                        doc /*
                        Compact representation of Project-level model synthesis.

                        Internally this behavior covers Project-level coverage assessment,
                        derivation-strategy selection, unified Model Candidate derivation,
                        Human Candidate Review, Model Placement, Human Model Placement
                        Review and deterministic Internal Engineering Model assembly.

                        These internal stages are intentionally collapsed into one node in
                        the presentation-oriented end-to-end data-flow view.
                        */


                        in item admittedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                            ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;

                        in item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        out item internalEngineeringModel :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel;


                        action assessCoverage :
                            '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                            'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency' {

                            in item admittedInformation :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                                ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;

                            out item coverageAssessment :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;
                        }


                        action selectStrategy :
                            '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                            'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {

                            in item admittedInformation :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                                ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;

                            out item derivationContext :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelDerivationContext;
                        }


                        action deriveCandidates :
                            '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                            'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {

                            in item admittedInformation :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                                ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;

                            in item coverageAssessment :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment;

                            in item derivationContext :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelDerivationContext;

                            out item modelCandidateSet :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;
                        }


                        action reviewCandidates :
                            '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                            'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                            in item modelCandidateSet :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;

                            out item candidateReviewDecision :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                        }


                        action derivePlacement :
                            '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                            'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {

                            in item modelCandidateSet :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;

                            in item candidateReviewDecision :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                            out item placementProposals :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelPlacementProposalSet;
                        }


                        action reviewPlacement :
                            '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                            'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                            in item placementProposals :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelPlacementProposalSet;

                            out item approvedPlacementSet :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedModelPlacementSet;
                        }


                        action assembleModel :
                            '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                            'Derive and Structure Architecture Information' {

                            in item admittedInformation :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::
                                ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet;

                            in item modelCandidateSet :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet;

                            in item approvedPlacementSet :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedModelPlacementSet;

                            out item internalEngineeringModel :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel;
                        }


                        first assessCoverage then selectStrategy;
                        first selectStrategy then deriveCandidates;
                        first deriveCandidates then reviewCandidates;
                        first reviewCandidates then derivePlacement;
                        first derivePlacement then reviewPlacement;
                        first reviewPlacement then assembleModel;


                        binding bind assessCoverage.admittedInformation = admittedInformation;
                        binding bind selectStrategy.admittedInformation = admittedInformation;
                        binding bind deriveCandidates.admittedInformation = admittedInformation;
                        binding bind assembleModel.admittedInformation = admittedInformation;

                        binding bind assembleModel.internalEngineeringModel =
                            internalEngineeringModel;


                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::CoverageAssessment
                            from assessCoverage.coverageAssessment
                            to deriveCandidates.coverageAssessment;


                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelDerivationContext
                            from selectStrategy.derivationContext
                            to deriveCandidates.derivationContext;


                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet
                            from deriveCandidates.modelCandidateSet
                            to reviewCandidates.modelCandidateSet;


                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet
                            from deriveCandidates.modelCandidateSet
                            to derivePlacement.modelCandidateSet;


                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelCandidateSet
                            from deriveCandidates.modelCandidateSet
                            to assembleModel.modelCandidateSet;


                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision
                            from reviewCandidates.candidateReviewDecision
                            to derivePlacement.candidateReviewDecision;


                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ModelPlacementProposalSet
                            from derivePlacement.placementProposals
                            to reviewPlacement.placementProposals;


                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedModelPlacementSet
                            from reviewPlacement.approvedPlacementSet
                            to assembleModel.approvedPlacementSet;
                    }


                    // ============================================================
                    // Collapsed publication behavior
                    // ============================================================

                    action def PublicationRelease {

                        doc /*
                        Compact representation of the final publication gate and
                        publication-evidence recording.

                        Publication authority depends on the exact generated artifact set,
                        its validation result and the corresponding Human Final Review
                        decision.
                        */


                        in item generatedArtifactSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;

                        in item validationResult :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;

                        in item finalReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                        out item publicationAuthorization :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization;


                        action enforcePublicationGate :
                            '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                            'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {

                            in item generatedArtifactSet :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;

                            in item validationResult :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;

                            in item finalReviewDecision :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;

                            out item publicationAuthorization :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization;
                        }


                        action recordPublication :
                            '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                            'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability' {

                            in item publicationAuthorization :
                                '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization;
                        }


                        first enforcePublicationGate then recordPublication;


                        binding bind enforcePublicationGate.generatedArtifactSet =
                            generatedArtifactSet;

                        binding bind enforcePublicationGate.validationResult =
                            validationResult;

                        binding bind enforcePublicationGate.finalReviewDecision =
                            finalReviewDecision;

                        binding bind enforcePublicationGate.publicationAuthorization =
                            publicationAuthorization;


                        flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::PublicationAuthorization
                            from enforcePublicationGate.publicationAuthorization
                            to recordPublication.publicationAuthorization;
                    }


                    // ============================================================
                    // Presentation-level nominal flow
                    // ============================================================

                    first start;
                    then registerSource;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Register source
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action registerSource :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context' {

                        in item engineeringSource :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringSourceArtifact;

                        out item registeredEngineeringSource :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringSourceArtifact;

                        out item projectAndSourceContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Configure run
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action configureRun :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs' {

                        in item projectAndSourceContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext;

                        out item processingRunContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext;

                        out item processingAttemptContext :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Detailed source processing
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action processInformation : SFB_005;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Human Engineering Review
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action reviewInformation :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        in item reviewPackage :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ReviewPackage;

                        out item approvedInformation :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Project Fit
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action projectFit : ProjectFitAdmission;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Project-level model derivation, placement and assembly
                    // collapsed into one presentation node
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action deriveArchitecture : ProjectModelSynthesis;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Internal Engineering Model assurance
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action validateArchitecture :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Validate and Integrate Architecture Content' {

                        in item internalEngineeringModel :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel;

                        out item validatedInternalModel :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ValidatedInternalEngineeringModel;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // SysML v2 generation
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action generateSysML :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Generate SysML v2 Architecture Artifacts' {

                        in item validatedInternalModel :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ValidatedInternalEngineeringModel;

                        out item generatedArtifactSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Separate generated-artifact validation
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action validateSysML :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts' {

                        in item generatedArtifactSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;

                        out item validationResult :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Final Human Model Review
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action finalReview :
                        '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::
                        'Support Human Review and Approval' {

                        in item generatedArtifactSet :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet;

                        in item validationResult :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult;

                        out item finalReviewDecision :
                            '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision;
                    }


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Publication
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    action publishModel : PublicationRelease;


                    then done;


                    // ============================================================
                    // External capability / Human involvement
                    // ============================================================

                    binding bind processInformation.llmService = llmService;

                    dependency from reviewInformation to humanReviewer;
                    dependency from finalReview to humanReviewer;

                    dependency from processInformation to llmService;


                    // ============================================================
                    // High-level engineering data flows
                    // ============================================================

                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext
                        from registerSource.projectAndSourceContext
                        to configureRun.projectAndSourceContext;


                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::EngineeringSourceArtifact
                        from registerSource.registeredEngineeringSource
                        to processInformation.engineeringSource;


                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext
                        from registerSource.projectAndSourceContext
                        to processInformation.projectAndSourceContext;


                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext
                        from configureRun.processingRunContext
                        to processInformation.processingRunContext;


                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ReviewPackage
                        from processInformation.reviewPackage
                        to reviewInformation.reviewPackage;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Project Fit
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SourceProjection
                        from processInformation.sourceProjection
                        to projectFit.sourceProjection;


                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ApprovedEngineeringInformation
                        from reviewInformation.approvedInformation
                        to projectFit.approvedInformation;


                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAndSourceContext
                        from registerSource.projectAndSourceContext
                        to projectFit.projectAndSourceContext;


                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext
                        from configureRun.processingRunContext
                        to projectFit.processingRunContext;


                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingAttemptContext
                        from configureRun.processingAttemptContext
                        to projectFit.processingAttemptContext;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Project-level model synthesis
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProjectAdmittedEngineeringInformationSet
                        from projectFit.admittedInformation
                        to deriveArchitecture.admittedInformation;


                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ProcessingRunContext
                        from configureRun.processingRunContext
                        to deriveArchitecture.processingRunContext;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // IEM assurance
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::InternalEngineeringModel
                        from deriveArchitecture.internalEngineeringModel
                        to validateArchitecture.internalEngineeringModel;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // SysML generation
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::ValidatedInternalEngineeringModel
                        from validateArchitecture.validatedInternalModel
                        to generateSysML.validatedInternalModel;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // SysML validation
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet
                        from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet
                        to validateSysML.generatedArtifactSet;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Final Human Review
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet
                        from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet
                        to finalReview.generatedArtifactSet;


                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult
                        from validateSysML.validationResult
                        to finalReview.validationResult;


                    // ------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Publication
                    // ------------------------------------------------------------

                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::GeneratedSysMLArtifactSet
                        from generateSysML.generatedArtifactSet
                        to publishModel.generatedArtifactSet;


                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::SysMLValidationResult
                        from validateSysML.validationResult
                        to publishModel.validationResult;


                    flow of '00_BehaviorDefinitions'::HumanReviewDecision
                        from finalReview.finalReviewDecision
                        to publishModel.finalReviewDecision;
                }
            }
        }
        package '02_TraceabilityViews' {

            doc /*
            Curated System Function interaction network.
            The dependencies represent principal functional interaction,
            information provision, orchestration influence, Human-review feedback,
            rework, evidence support and workflow-state projection.
            They do not prescribe one mandatory temporal execution sequence and
            do not define Logical Components or subsystem boundaries.
            Detailed item flows and control behavior remain authoritative in
            01_SystemBehavior.
            */

            package '00_FunctionInteractionNetwork' {
                doc /*
                Presentation-oriented interaction network for the thirteen accepted
                System Functions.
                The network emphasizes the principal engineering-information path,
                Human-authority boundaries, Project Fit, model synthesis,
                SysML v2 generation and validation, evidence support and
                workflow-state projection.
                */

                // ============================================================
                // Project and source context
                // ============================================================
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information';

                // ============================================================
                // Source-local engineering-information processing
                // ============================================================

                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Apply Semantic Governance';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Apply Semantic Governance'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate and Compare Engineering Candidates';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate and Compare Engineering Candidates'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval';

                // Human-requested source-local rework.
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Apply Semantic Governance';

                // ============================================================
                // Project Fit and Project-level synthesis
                // ============================================================

                // Project Fit assessment is performed on Human-approved
                // source-local engineering information.
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information';

                // Project Fit enforcement / Project admission is orchestrated.
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';

                // Candidate and Model Placement Human-review boundary.
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';

                // ============================================================
                // Internal Engineering Model assurance
                // ============================================================

                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate and Integrate Architecture Content';

                // Architecture-level rework.
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate and Integrate Architecture Content'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';

                // ============================================================
                // SysML v2 generation and validation
                // ============================================================

                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate and Integrate Architecture Content'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate SysML v2 Architecture Artifacts';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate SysML v2 Architecture Artifacts'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts';

                // Representation-level correction may require regeneration.
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate SysML v2 Architecture Artifacts';

                // A successfully validated exact artifact set proceeds to
                // Final Human Model Review.
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval';

                // Final Human approval feeds the publication gate.
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';

                // ============================================================
                // Cross-cutting evidence and traceability
                // ============================================================

                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Apply Semantic Governance'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate and Compare Engineering Candidates'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate and Integrate Architecture Content'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate SysML v2 Architecture Artifacts'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';

                // Persisted evidence supports Human review and coverage assessment.
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency';

                // ============================================================
                // Engineer-facing workflow projection
                // ============================================================

                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Present Processing Status and History';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Present Processing Status and History';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Present Processing Status and History';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Present Processing Status and History';
                dependency
                    from '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Present Processing Status and History';
            }

            view GV_SystemFunctionNetworkPlan : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::gv {
