        package '03_ActionFlowViews' {

            doc /*
            Curated views of the System Functional behavior.

            The presentation-oriented views intentionally expose only selected
            behavior elements in order to communicate the engineering-data path
            through the Turing Generator without reproducing the complete control,
            authority, rework and recovery lifecycle.

            Detailed behavior remains modeled in 01_SystemBehavior.
            */

            // ================================================================
            // Technical lifecycle view
            // ================================================================

            view AFV_EngineeringTransformationLifecycle_Overview : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::afv {

                doc /*
                Technical overview of the Engineering Transformation Lifecycle.
                The view exposes SFB_002 as the authoritative control-oriented lifecycle
                for source-local processing, Human review, Project Fit, Project-level
                model synthesis, Model Placement, deterministic model assembly,
                SysML v2 generation and validation, Final Human Model Review and
                publication.
                Rework paths and lifecycle decisions are retained because they are part
                of the authoritative control behavior.
                */
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'02_EngineeringLifecycle'::SFB_002;
            }
            // ================================================================
            // PRESENTATION VIEW 1
            // Compact end-to-end engineering-data transformation
            // ================================================================

            view GV_EngineeringDataTransformationFlow : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::gv {

                doc /*
                Presentation-oriented end-to-end engineering-data flow.
                The view shows the principal transformation stages from a registered
                Engineering Source to publication of the generated SysML v2 model.
                Detailed source processing is collapsed into Process Information.
                Model Candidate derivation, Human Candidate Review, Model Placement,
                Human Model Placement Review and deterministic model assembly are
                intentionally collapsed into Derive Architecture.
                Detailed lifecycle gates, rework and recovery are intentionally
                omitted.
                */
                expose Actions::Action::start;
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Source and processing setup
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::registerSource;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::configureRun;
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Source-local information processing
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::processInformation;
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Human Engineering Review
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::reviewInformation;
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Project admission
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::projectFit;
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Collapsed Project-level model synthesis
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::deriveArchitecture;
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Internal Engineering Model assurance
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::validateArchitecture;
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // SysML v2 generation and separate validation
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::generateSysML;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::validateSysML;
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Final Human Review and publication
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::finalReview;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::publishModel;
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Supporting external participants
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::llmService;
                expose '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_004::humanReviewer;
                expose Actions::Action::done;
            }
            // ================================================================
            // PRESENTATION VIEW 2
            // Zoom into source-local Engineering Information Processing
            // ================================================================

            view GV_EngineeringInformationProcessingFlow
                : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::afv {

                doc /*
                Presentation-oriented Action Flow View of source-local Engineering
                Information Processing.

                The view shows deterministic Source Projection, Source Analysis Units,
                source-grounded Evidence Detection, Canonical Engineering Subject
                discovery, Persona Interpretation, consensus and variance assessment,
                controlled semantic governance, coverage and evidence assessment and
                preparation of the Human Engineering Review package.

                Project Fit and Project-level model synthesis are intentionally outside
                this view.

                The enclosing SFB_005 behavior is exposed instead of individually
                exposing its nested action usages.
                */

                expose
                    '01_SystemBehavior'::'04_EngineeringDataFlow'::SFB_005;
            }
        }
    }
    package '03_Logical' {
        package '00_LogicalDefinitions' {
            part def LogicalComponent;
            part def ExternalArchitectureContext;
            part def <EC_01> 'LLM Inference Context' :> ExternalArchitectureContext {
                doc
                /* External LLM inference capability used by selected logical responsibilities.
                 * It supports engineering processing but has no engineering approval authority.
                 */
            }
            part def <EC_02> 'Semantic Reference Knowledge' :> ExternalArchitectureContext {
                doc
                /* Curated semantic reference context including the Turing Core Vocabulary,
                 * BFO 2020, IOF Core and the derived Reference Concept Index.
                 *
                 * Reference knowledge supports terminology and semantic consistency.
                 * It does not create or approve authoritative engineering information.
                 */
            }
            part def <EC_03> 'Target Model and Framework References' :> ExternalArchitectureContext {
                doc
                /* Versioned framework, target-model and projection references constraining
                 * architecture derivation and target-model structuring.
                 */
            }
            part def <EC_04> 'SysML v2 Modeling References' :> ExternalArchitectureContext {
                doc
                /* Controlled SysML v2 language, syntax, validation and example-model references.
                 *
                 * Includes the SysML v2 specification/release material, validated SYSIDE
                 * syntax evidence and reviewed non-normative example-model references such
                 * as Apollo 11.
                 */
            }
            port def LogicalInteractionPort;
            interface def LogicalInteraction {
                end sourceEnd : LogicalInteractionPort;
                end targetEnd : LogicalInteractionPort;
            }
        }
        package '01_LogicalComponents' {

            part def <LC_01> 'User Interaction and Status Presentation'
                :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {

                doc /*
                Provides the human-facing interaction boundary for project and source
                navigation, processing initiation, review interaction, status,
                attention conditions and retained-history presentation.
                */

                port projectNavigationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port orchestrationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port reviewPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port statusPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;

                dependency
                    from 'User Interaction and Status Presentation'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Present Processing Status and History';
            }
            part def <LC_02> 'Project and Source Context Management'
                :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {
                doc /*
                Maintains project identity, project isolation, active project context,
                source registration, source identity and source selection.
                */

                port userInteractionPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port orchestrationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port sourceProcessingPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                dependency
                    from 'Project and Source Context Management'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Manage Engineering Project and Source Context';
            }
            part def <LC_03> 'Processing Orchestration and State Control'
                :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {

                doc /*
                Configures and coordinates processing runs, invokes selected stages,
                enforces suitability, Project Fit, Human-review and final-publication
                gates, controls run-state transitions and coordinates retry, recovery
                and successor-run behavior.
                */

                port userInteractionPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port projectContextPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port informationProcessingPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port reviewGovernancePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port architecturePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port artifactGenerationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port artifactValidationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port evidencePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                dependency
                    from 'Processing Orchestration and State Control'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Configure and Orchestrate Processing Runs';
            }
            part def <LC_04> 'Engineering Information Processing'
                :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {
                doc /*
                Creates processable source projections, assesses and prepares
                source-local engineering information, preserves Engineering Source
                purity, supports controlled completion, applies semantic governance
                and assesses Project Source admissibility before project-level
                synthesis.
                */
                port sourceContextPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port orchestrationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port candidateReviewPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port evidencePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                dependency
                    from 'Engineering Information Processing'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess and Prepare Source Information';
                dependency
                    from 'Engineering Information Processing'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Apply Semantic Governance';
            }

            part def <LC_05> 'Candidate and Review Governance'
                :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {

                doc /*
                Generates and compares engineering interpretations and supports the
                Human-authority boundaries for source-local Engineering Review,
                Model Candidate Review, Model Placement authorization and Final
                Human Model Review.
                It presents review evidence, captures Human decisions, binds those
                decisions to exact reviewed content and controls promotion across
                Human-authorized engineering boundaries.
,                */

                port userInteractionPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port orchestrationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port informationProcessingPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port architecturePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port artifactValidationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port evidencePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                dependency
                    from 'Candidate and Review Governance'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate and Compare Engineering Candidates';
                dependency
                    from 'Candidate and Review Governance'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Support Human Review and Approval';
            }

            part def <LC_06> 'Coverage Evidence and Traceability Management'
                :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {
                doc /*
                Assesses engineering-information coverage and evidence sufficiency
                and maintains processing evidence, Project Fit evidence, processing
                history, Human decisions, source and result provenance, source-local
                engineering authority traceability, multi-source subject identity
                and artifact-integrity references.
                */

                port userInteractionPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port orchestrationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port informationProcessingPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port reviewGovernancePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port architecturePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port artifactGenerationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port artifactValidationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                dependency
                    from 'Coverage Evidence and Traceability Management'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Assess Coverage and Evidence Sufficiency';
                dependency
                    from 'Coverage Evidence and Traceability Management'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Maintain Processing Evidence and Traceability';
            }
            part def <LC_07> 'Architecture Synthesis and Validation'
                :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {
                doc /*
                Derives one Project-level Model Candidate Set from admitted
                source-local engineering authority inputs, applies the selected
                derivation strategy, derives Model Placement proposals and
                deterministically assembles the Internal Engineering Model after
                Human-authorized placement.
                It subsequently assesses architecture-level consistency,
                constraints, interfaces and integration compatibility before
                SysML v2 representation generation.
                */

                port orchestrationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port reviewGovernancePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port artifactGenerationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port evidencePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                dependency
                    from 'Architecture Synthesis and Validation'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Derive and Structure Architecture Information';
                dependency
                    from 'Architecture Synthesis and Validation'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate and Integrate Architecture Content';
            }
            part def <LC_08> 'SysML v2 Artifact Generation'
                :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {
                doc /*
                Deterministically generates the traceable Architecture-as-Code
                artifact set in the supported SysML v2 textual representation,
                organizes the generated artifacts according to the selected target
                structure and identifies unsupported representation content.
                Generated-artifact validation is a separate logical responsibility.
                */

                port orchestrationPort :'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port architecturePort :'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port validationPort :'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port evidencePort :'00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                dependency
                    from 'SysML v2 Artifact Generation'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Generate SysML v2 Architecture Artifacts';
            }
            part def <LC_09> 'Generated SysML v2 Validation'
                :> '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalComponent {
                doc /*
                Validates the exact generated SysML v2 artifact set, records
                validation findings and publication-blocking status and preserves
                the generated artifact set without modification during validation.
                This responsibility is logically separated from SysML v2 artifact
                generation and provides the validation result required by Final
                Human Model Review and publication control.
                */
                port orchestrationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port artifactGenerationPort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port reviewGovernancePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                port evidencePort : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteractionPort;
                dependency
                    from 'Generated SysML v2 Validation'
                    to '02_System'::'02_Functional'::'00_FunctionDefinitions'::'Validate Generated SysML v2 Artifacts';
            }
        }
        package '02_LogicalArchitecture' {
            part <LA_001> 'Turing Generator Logical Architecture' {

                // ============================================================
                // Logical Components
                // ============================================================
                part userInteraction : '01_LogicalComponents'::'User Interaction and Status Presentation';
                part projectSourceManagement : '01_LogicalComponents'::'Project and Source Context Management';
                part processingOrchestration : '01_LogicalComponents'::'Processing Orchestration and State Control';
                part informationProcessing : '01_LogicalComponents'::'Engineering Information Processing';
                part candidateReviewGovernance : '01_LogicalComponents'::'Candidate and Review Governance';
                part evidenceTraceability : '01_LogicalComponents'::'Coverage Evidence and Traceability Management';
                part architectureSynthesis : '01_LogicalComponents'::'Architecture Synthesis and Validation';
                part sysmlArtifactGeneration : '01_LogicalComponents'::'SysML v2 Artifact Generation';
                part sysmlArtifactValidation : '01_LogicalComponents'::'Generated SysML v2 Validation';

                // ============================================================
                // External Human and knowledge contexts
                // ============================================================

                ref part humanReviewer : '01_Stakeholder'::'00_Stakeholders'::HumanRole;
                ref part llmInference : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::'LLM Inference Context';
                ref part semanticReferenceKnowledge : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::'Semantic Reference Knowledge';
                ref part targetModelReferences : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::'Target Model and Framework References';
                ref part sysmlModelingReferences : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::'SysML v2 Modeling References';

                // Human interaction and Human engineering authority.
                dependency from userInteraction to humanReviewer;
                dependency from candidateReviewGovernance to humanReviewer;

                // LLM-supported logical responsibilities.
                dependency from informationProcessing to llmInference;
                dependency from candidateReviewGovernance to llmInference;
                dependency from architectureSynthesis to llmInference;

                // Controlled knowledge/reference contexts.
                dependency from informationProcessing
                    to semanticReferenceKnowledge;
                dependency from architectureSynthesis
                    to targetModelReferences;
                dependency from sysmlArtifactGeneration
                    to sysmlModelingReferences;
                dependency from sysmlArtifactValidation
                    to sysmlModelingReferences;

                // ============================================================
                // Human-facing logical interactions
                // ============================================================
                interface projectNavigationExchange : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> userInteraction.projectNavigationPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> projectSourceManagement.userInteractionPort;
                interface processingInteraction : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> userInteraction.orchestrationPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> processingOrchestration.userInteractionPort;
                interface reviewInteraction : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> userInteraction.reviewPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> candidateReviewGovernance.userInteractionPort;
                interface statusAndHistoryPresentation : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> evidenceTraceability.userInteractionPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> userInteraction.statusPort;

                // ============================================================
                // Project context and source-local processing
                // ============================================================

                interface projectRunContextExchange : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> projectSourceManagement.orchestrationPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> processingOrchestration.projectContextPort;
                interface sourceContextExchange : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> projectSourceManagement.sourceProcessingPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> informationProcessing.sourceContextPort;
                interface informationProcessingControl : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> processingOrchestration.informationProcessingPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> informationProcessing.orchestrationPort;

                // Project Fit is assessed by Engineering Information Processing
                // and returned to orchestration for fail-closed admission control.
                interface projectFitAssessmentExchange : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> informationProcessing.orchestrationPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> processingOrchestration.informationProcessingPort;

                // ============================================================
                // Human Engineering Review
                // ============================================================

                interface reviewGateControl : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> processingOrchestration.reviewGovernancePort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> candidateReviewGovernance.orchestrationPort;
                interface preparedInformationAndFindings : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> informationProcessing.candidateReviewPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> candidateReviewGovernance.informationProcessingPort;
                interface reviewFeedback : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> candidateReviewGovernance.informationProcessingPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> informationProcessing.candidateReviewPort;

                // ============================================================
                // Project admission and Model Synthesis
                // ============================================================

                // Project-admitted source-local engineering authority information
                // is provided by orchestration to Project-level model synthesis.
                interface architectureStageControlAndAdmission : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> processingOrchestration.architecturePort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> architectureSynthesis.orchestrationPort;

                // Model Candidate Sets and Model Placement proposals are provided
                // to the Human-review responsibility.
                interface modelReviewSubjectExchange : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> architectureSynthesis.reviewGovernancePort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> candidateReviewGovernance.architecturePort;

                // Human Candidate and Placement decisions return to model synthesis.
                interface modelReviewDecisionExchange : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> candidateReviewGovernance.architecturePort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> architectureSynthesis.reviewGovernancePort;

                // ============================================================
                // Internal Engineering Model → SysML v2 generation
                // ============================================================

                interface artifactGenerationStageControl : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> processingOrchestration.artifactGenerationPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> sysmlArtifactGeneration.orchestrationPort;
                interface validatedInternalModelExchange : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> architectureSynthesis.artifactGenerationPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> sysmlArtifactGeneration.architecturePort;

                // Generation-level representation findings may require
                // architecture-side correction.
                interface representationGenerationFeedback : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> sysmlArtifactGeneration.architecturePort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> architectureSynthesis.artifactGenerationPort;

                // ============================================================
                // Separate SysML v2 artifact validation
                // ============================================================

                interface artifactValidationStageControl : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> processingOrchestration.artifactValidationPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> sysmlArtifactValidation.orchestrationPort;

                // Exact generated artifact set enters the independent
                // validation responsibility.
                interface generatedArtifactSetForValidation : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> sysmlArtifactGeneration.validationPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> sysmlArtifactValidation.artifactGenerationPort;

                // Failed validation may require deterministic regeneration.
                interface validationFeedback : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> sysmlArtifactValidation.artifactGenerationPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> sysmlArtifactGeneration.validationPort;

                // Validation status is also required by the publication gate.
                interface validationGateStatusExchange : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> sysmlArtifactValidation.orchestrationPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> processingOrchestration.artifactValidationPort;

                // ============================================================
                // Final Human Model Review
                // ============================================================

                // Final Human Model Review operates on the exact generated
                // artifact set...
                interface generatedArtifactSetForFinalReview : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> sysmlArtifactGeneration.validationPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> candidateReviewGovernance.artifactValidationPort;

                // ...together with its separate validation result.
                interface sysmlValidationResultForFinalReview : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> sysmlArtifactValidation.reviewGovernancePort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> candidateReviewGovernance.artifactValidationPort;

                // Final Human approval is returned to orchestration, where the
                // publication gate remains authoritative.
                interface finalReviewDecisionExchange : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> candidateReviewGovernance.orchestrationPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> processingOrchestration.reviewGovernancePort;

                // The generated artifact set is retained by orchestration for
                // exact-subject publication control.
                interface generatedArtifactSetForPublication : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> sysmlArtifactGeneration.orchestrationPort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> processingOrchestration.artifactGenerationPort;

                // ============================================================
                // Cross-cutting processing evidence and traceability
                // ============================================================
                interface processingEvidenceControl : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> processingOrchestration.evidencePort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> evidenceTraceability.orchestrationPort;
                interface informationProcessingEvidence : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> informationProcessing.evidencePort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> evidenceTraceability.informationProcessingPort;
                interface reviewDecisionEvidence : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> candidateReviewGovernance.evidencePort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> evidenceTraceability.reviewGovernancePort;
                interface architectureEvidence : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> architectureSynthesis.evidencePort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> evidenceTraceability.architecturePort;
                interface artifactGenerationEvidence : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> sysmlArtifactGeneration.evidencePort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> evidenceTraceability.artifactGenerationPort;
                interface artifactValidationEvidence : '00_LogicalDefinitions'::LogicalInteraction
                    connect
                        sourceEnd ::> sysmlArtifactValidation.evidencePort
                        to
                        targetEnd ::> evidenceTraceability.artifactValidationPort;
            }
        }
        package '03_Views' {
            doc
            /* Curated logical interconnection view.
             *
             * Only the eight Logical Component usages are exposed as diagram nodes.
             * The interface usages remain contained in the Logical Architecture and
             * shall be rendered as in-between relations between the exposed parts.
             */
            view IV_TuringGeneratorLogicalArchitecture : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::iv {
                expose TuringGeneratorLogicalArchitecture_Presentation::userInteraction;
                expose TuringGeneratorLogicalArchitecture_Presentation::projectSourceManagement;
                expose TuringGeneratorLogicalArchitecture_Presentation::processingOrchestration;
                expose TuringGeneratorLogicalArchitecture_Presentation::informationProcessing;
                expose TuringGeneratorLogicalArchitecture_Presentation::candidateReviewGovernance;
                expose TuringGeneratorLogicalArchitecture_Presentation::evidenceTraceability;
                expose TuringGeneratorLogicalArchitecture_Presentation::architectureSynthesis;
                expose TuringGeneratorLogicalArchitecture_Presentation::sysmlArtifactGeneration;
                expose TuringGeneratorLogicalArchitecture_Presentation::sysmlArtifactValidation;
            }
            view IV_TuringGeneratorLogicalArchitecture_Overview : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::iv;
            view TuringGeneratorLogicalArchitecture_Presentation : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::iv {
                doc
                /* Curated presentation view of the Turing Generator Logical Architecture.
                 *
                 * Shows the eight logical responsibilities together with selected
                 * external Human, LLM, semantic-reference, target-model and SysML v2
                 * modeling contexts.
                 *
                 * Only presentation-relevant logical interactions are exposed.
                 */
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::userInteraction;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::projectSourceManagement;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::processingOrchestration;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::informationProcessing;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::candidateReviewGovernance;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::evidenceTraceability;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::architectureSynthesis;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::sysmlArtifactGeneration;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::sysmlArtifactValidation;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::humanReviewer;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::llmInference;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::semanticReferenceKnowledge;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::targetModelReferences;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::sysmlModelingReferences;

                // ============================================================
                // Presentation-relevant logical interactions
                // ============================================================

                // Human-facing interaction.
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::projectNavigationExchange;

                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::reviewInteraction;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::statusAndHistoryPresentation;

                // Project context and source-local processing.
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::projectRunContextExchange;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::sourceContextExchange;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::informationProcessingControl;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::projectFitAssessmentExchange;

                // Human Engineering Review.
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::reviewGateControl;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::preparedInformationAndFindings;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::reviewFeedback;

                // Project-level model synthesis and Human authority.
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::architectureStageControlAndAdmission;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::modelReviewSubjectExchange;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::modelReviewDecisionExchange;

                // Internal Engineering Model and SysML v2 generation.
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::artifactGenerationStageControl;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::validatedInternalModelExchange;

                // Generated SysML v2 validation.
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::artifactValidationStageControl;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::generatedArtifactSetForValidation;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::validationGateStatusExchange;

                // Final Human Model Review and publication.
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::generatedArtifactSetForFinalReview;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::sysmlValidationResultForFinalReview;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::finalReviewDecisionExchange;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::generatedArtifactSetForPublication;

                // Cross-cutting evidence and traceability.
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::processingEvidenceControl;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::informationProcessingEvidence;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::reviewDecisionEvidence;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::architectureEvidence;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::artifactGenerationEvidence;
                expose '02_LogicalArchitecture'::LA_001::artifactValidationEvidence;
                            }
            view GV_ThreeLayerArchitectureMapping : DS_Views::SymbolicViews::gv {
                doc
                /* Presentation-oriented mapping of the literature-derived
                 * Data, Process and Knowledge Layers to the accepted
                 * Logical Components of the Turing Generator.
                 */
                expose '99_ArchitectureLayers'::LAYER_01;
                expose '99_ArchitectureLayers'::LAYER_02;
                expose '99_ArchitectureLayers'::LAYER_03;

                expose '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_08;
                expose '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_09;
            }

        package '99_ArchitectureLayers' {
            part def ArchitectureLayer;
            part def <LAYER_01> 'Data Layer' :> ArchitectureLayer {
                doc
                /* Responsibility layer for acquisition, normalization, preparation,
                 * context, evidence and traceability of engineering information.
                 */
                dependency from __unnamed1 to '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_02;
                dependency __unnamed1 from LAYER_01 to '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_04;
                dependency from LAYER_01 to '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_06;
            }
            part def <LAYER_02> 'Process Layer' :> ArchitectureLayer {
                doc
                /* Responsibility layer for workflow control, candidate generation,
                 * Human Review, architecture derivation, validation and SysML v2
                 * artifact generation.
                 */
                dependency from LAYER_02 to '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_01;
                dependency from LAYER_02 to '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_03;
                dependency from LAYER_02 to '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_05;
                dependency from LAYER_02 to '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_07;
                dependency from LAYER_02 to '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_08;
            }
            part def <LAYER_03> 'Knowledge Layer' :> ArchitectureLayer {
                doc
                /* Cross-cutting governance layer providing semantic, reference,
                 * validation and synthesis knowledge across the architecture.
                 */
                dependency from LAYER_03 to '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_04;
                dependency from LAYER_03 to '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_05;
                dependency from LAYER_03 to '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_06;
                dependency from LAYER_03 to '01_LogicalComponents'::LC_07;
            }
        }
    }
